u_{\parallel}
and
v_{\parallel}
as a set of basis, the two vectors
\xi_{\parallel}
and
\eta_{\parallel}
are linearly independent. Similarly, we can infer that under the basis
u_{\perp},v_{\perp}
, vectors
\xi_{\perp}
and
\eta_{\perp}
are also linearly independent.

By comparing the expressions for
\xi_{\parallel}
and
\eta_{\parallel}
in (
2.34
) and (
2.45
), one can see that there exist constants
0<c_{v_{\parallel}},c_{u_{\parallel}}<1
such that

\begin{split}c_{v_{\parallel}}\big(\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}+\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}}{2}\big)=&\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}-\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}}{2},\\
c_{u_{\parallel}}\big(\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}}{2}+\frac{\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}\big)=&\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}}{2}-\frac{\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}.\end{split}

(2.47)

From (
2.46
), it can be obtained that

\begin{split}c_{u,-}:=&c_{v_{\parallel}}\big(\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}}{2}+\frac{\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}\big)-\big(\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}}{2}-\frac{\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}\big)>0,\\
c_{v,-}:=&\big(\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}-\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}}{2}\big)-c_{u_{\parallel}}\big(\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}+\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}}{2}\big)>0.\end{split}

We denote

\begin{split}\mathbf{c}_{u,+}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(c_{v_{\parallel}}\xi_{\parallel}+\eta_{\parallel}),\qquad\mathbf{c}_{u,-}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(c_{v_{\parallel}}\xi_{\parallel}-\eta_{\parallel})=\frac{c_{u,-}}{\sqrt{2}}u_{\parallel},\\
\mathbf{c}_{v,+}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(c_{u_{\parallel}}\xi_{\parallel}+\eta_{\parallel}),\qquad\mathbf{c}_{v,-}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\eta_{\parallel}-c_{u_{\parallel}}\xi_{\parallel})=\frac{c_{v,-}}{\sqrt{2}}v_{\parallel},\end{split}

which gives the equality that

\begin{split}(c_{v_{\parallel}})^{2}|\xi_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\eta_{\parallel}|^{2}=|\mathbf{c}_{u,+}|^{2}+|\mathbf{c}_{u,-}|^{2}=|\mathbf{c}_{u,+}|^{2}+\frac{(c_{u,-})^{2}}{2}|u_{\parallel}|^{2},\\
(c_{u_{\parallel}})^{2}|\xi_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\eta_{\parallel}|^{2}=|\mathbf{c}_{v,+}|^{2}+|\mathbf{c}_{v,-}|^{2}=|\mathbf{c}_{v,+}|^{2}+\frac{(c_{v,-})^{2}}{2}|v_{\parallel}|^{2}.\end{split}

Similarly, it can be observed that

\big(\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}-\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}}{2}\big):\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}}{2}<\big(\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}+\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}}{2}\big):\frac{\sqrt{m^{\beta}}}{2}.

Through a process analogous to the above that there exist constants

0<c_{v_{\perp}}<1
and
\tilde{c}_{u,-},\tilde{c}_{v,-}>0
, as well as vectors
\tilde{\mathbf{c}}_{u,+},\tilde{\mathbf{c}}_{v,+}
, such that

\begin{split}(c_{v_{\perp}})^{2}|\xi_{\perp}|^{2}+|\eta_{\perp}|^{2}&=|\mathbf{c}_{u,+}|^{2}+|\mathbf{c}_{u,-}|^{2}=|\mathbf{c}_{u,+}|^{2}+\frac{(c_{u,-})^{2}}{2}|u_{\perp}|^{2},\\
|\xi_{\perp}|^{2}+|\eta_{\perp}|^{2}&=|\mathbf{c}_{v,+}|^{2}+|\mathbf{c}_{v,-}|^{2}=|\mathbf{c}_{v,+}|^{2}+\frac{(c_{v,-})^{2}}{2}|v_{\perp}|^{2}.\end{split}

The exponential term about
\xi_{\parallel},\xi_{\perp},\eta_{\parallel},\eta_{\perp}
, in (
2.35
) can be bounded as follows:

e^{-\frac{1}{4}\big(|\xi_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\xi_{\perp}|^{2}+|\eta_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\eta_{\perp}|^{2}\big)}\leq e^{-\bar{c}\big(|v_{\parallel}|^{2}+|v_{\perp}|^{2}+|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\big)},

(2.48)

where

\bar{c}=\frac{1}{64}\min\Big\{\frac{1}{4},(c_{u,-})^{2},(c_{v,-})^{2},(\tilde{c}_{u,-})^{2},(\tilde{c}_{v,-})^{2}\Big\}>0.

Since
u=v_{*}-v
, we partition the integration domain
(|v|+|v_{*}|\geq m)
as follows:

\big(|v|+|u+v|\geq m\big)\subseteq\big(|v|+|u|\geq\frac{m}{2}\big)\subseteq\Big[(|v|\geq\frac{m}{4})\cup(|u|\geq\frac{m}{4})\Big],

which could further be split into

\begin{split}\big(|v|\geq\frac{m}{4}\big)&=\Big[(|v|\geq\frac{m}{4})\cap(|v_{\parallel}|\geq\frac{|v|}{2})\Big]\cup\Big[(|v|\geq\frac{m}{4})\cap(|v_{\perp}|\geq\frac{|v|}{2})\Big],\\
\big(|u|\geq\frac{m}{4}\big)&=\Big[(|u|\geq\frac{m}{4})\cap(|u_{\parallel}|\geq\frac{|u|}{2})\Big]\cup\Big[(|u|\geq\frac{m}{4})\cap(|u_{\perp}|\geq\frac{|u|}{2})\Big].\end{split}

Now, we consider the decay estimate for the part of (
2.35
).

Case B1.
\big(|v|\geq\frac{m}{4}\big)\cap\big(|v_{\parallel}|\geq\frac{|v|}{2}\big)
.

In this case
|v_{\parallel}|\geq\frac{m}{8}
, so we get

e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{4}|v_{\parallel}|^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{m},\hskip 54.06006pte^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{4}[|v_{\parallel}|^{2}+|v_{\perp}|^{2}]}\leq Cw^{\gamma}(v).

(2.49)

Then, the part about (
2.35
) in
\left\langle w^{2l}K_{2,r}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}_{1},f^{\alpha}_{2}\right\rangle_{v}
is bounded by

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\bar{c}(|v_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\parallel}|^{2})}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)w^{2l}(v)f^{\alpha}(v)

\displaystyle\hskip 39.83368pt\times e^{-\bar{c}(|v_{\perp}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2})}\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}f^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\,du_{\perp}du_{\parallel}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{C}{m}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}(|v_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\parallel}|^{2})}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)w^{2l+\gamma}(v)f^{\alpha}(v)

\displaystyle\hskip 45.52458pt\times e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}(|v_{\perp}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2})}\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}f^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\,du_{\perp}du_{\parallel}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{C_{\varepsilon}}{m}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w^{2l+\gamma}(v)

\displaystyle\hskip 162.18062pt\times\big[f^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\big]^{2}dvdu_{\parallel}du_{\perp}\Bigg)^{1/2}

(2.50)

\displaystyle\hskip 65.44142pt\times\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}|u_{\perp}|^{2}}du_{\perp}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}}du_{\parallel}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w^{2l+\gamma}(v)[f^{\alpha}(v)]^{2}dv\Bigg)^{1/2}

(2.51)

Because of

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}|u_{\perp}|^{2}}du_{\perp}\leq C,\qquad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}}du_{\parallel}\leq C,

clearly (
2.51
) is bounded by
|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{2}|_{\nu}
.

By the inequality that

w^{2l+\gamma}(v)\leq Cw^{2l+\gamma}\big(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel}\big)w^{2l+\gamma}(u_{\perp})w^{2l+\gamma}\big(\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel}\big),

thus (
2.50
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\varepsilon}}{m}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}w^{2l+\gamma}(u_{\perp})e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}|u_{\perp}|^{2}}du_{\perp}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}w^{2l+\gamma}(\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}}du_{\parallel}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}w^{2l+\gamma}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})[f^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})]^{2}dv\Bigg)^{1/2}

(2.52)

Since

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}w^{2l+\gamma}(u_{\perp})e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}|u_{\perp}|^{2}}du_{\perp}\leq C,\qquad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}w^{2l+\gamma}(\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{2}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}}du_{\parallel}\leq C,

(
2.50
) can be further bounded by
\frac{C_{\varepsilon}}{m}|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{1}|_{\nu}
.

Consequently, the part about (
2.35
) in
\left\langle w^{2l}K_{2,r}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}_{1},f^{\alpha}_{2}\right\rangle_{v}
is bounded by
\frac{C_{\varepsilon}}{m}|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{1}|_{\nu}|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{2}|_{\nu}
.

Case B2.
\big(|v|\geq\frac{m}{4}\big)\cap\big(|v_{\perp}|\geq\frac{|v|}{2}\big)
.

In this case
|v_{\perp}|\geq\frac{m}{8}>1
, then we have

e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{4}|v_{\perp}|^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{m},\hskip 54.06006pte^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{4}(|v_{\parallel}|^{2}+|v_{\perp}|^{2})}\leq Cw^{\gamma}(v).

(2.53)

Case B3.
\big(|u|\geq\frac{m}{4}\big)\cap\big(|u_{\parallel}|\geq\frac{|u|}{2}\big)
.

In this case
|u_{\parallel}|\geq\frac{m}{8}>1
, then we have

e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{4}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{m},\hskip 54.06006pte^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{4}(|v_{\parallel}|^{2}+|v_{\perp}|^{2})}\leq Cw^{\gamma}(v).

(2.54)

Case B4.
\big(|u|\geq\frac{m}{4}\big)\cap\big(|u_{\perp}|\geq\frac{|u|}{2}\big)
.

In this case
|u_{\perp}|\geq\frac{m}{8}>1
, then we have

e^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{4}|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{m},\hskip 54.06006pte^{-\frac{\bar{c}}{4}(|v_{\parallel}|^{2}+|v_{\perp}|^{2})}\leq Cw^{\gamma}(v).

(2.55)

By replacing (
2.49
) with (
2.53
) or (
2.54
) or (
2.55
), we follow the analysis process in Case B1. For Case B2, Case B3, and Case B4, the same conclusion as in Case B1 can be concluded.

So we conclude the estimates for the part of (
2.35
) with
m^{\alpha}\neq m^{\beta}
in
\left\langle w^{2l}K_{2,r}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}_{1},f^{\alpha}_{2}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}
.

The degenerate part*
.  Next, we turn to the case of
\alpha=\beta
.

(1)
For the part of
k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1),r}
, the proof for case
\alpha\neq\beta
can be fully applied to the proof for case
\alpha=\beta
, yielding the same conclusion.

(2)^{*}
The integration kernel‌
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2),r}
no longer adheres to exponential decay (
2.36
) with (
2.48
),
but instead satisfies the decay analogous to
k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1),r}
.

Proof.

Notice by (
2.34
) and (
2.45
), when
\alpha=\beta
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\xi_{\parallel}&=\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}(v_{\parallel}+u_{\parallel}),\hskip 26.46085pt\eta_{\parallel}=0\\
\xi_{\perp}&=\sqrt{m^{\alpha}}(v_{\perp}+\frac{1}{2}u_{\perp}),\hskip 14.22636pt\eta_{\perp}=\frac{1}{2}u_{\perp}\end{split}

Therefore,
\xi_{\parallel}
and
\eta_{\parallel}
are not linearly independent. We cannot obtain exponential decay for
v_{\parallel}
and
u_{\parallel}
individually; only exhibits exponential decay with respect to
(v_{\parallel}+u_{\parallel})
. This results in
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2),r}
no longer possessing exponential decay with respect to
v
.

The part of
\alpha=\beta
in (
2.23
) is

\displaystyle\Bigg(\int_{|v|+|v_{*}|>m}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|u|^{\gamma}[\mu^{\alpha}(u+v)]^{\frac{1}{2}}\chi(|u|)\big[\mu^{\alpha}(v+u_{\perp})\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}

(2.56)

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt\times f^{\alpha}_{1}(v+u_{\parallel})b^{\alpha\alpha}(\theta)\,du\,d\omega

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{|v|+|v_{*}|>m}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|u|^{\gamma}[\mu^{\alpha}(u+v)]^{\frac{1}{2}}\chi(|u|)\big[\mu^{\alpha}(v+u_{\parallel})\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}

(2.57)

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt\times f^{\alpha}_{1}(v+u_{\perp})b^{\alpha\alpha}(\theta)\,du\,d\omega\Bigg)

where (
2.56
) is the part for
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(1),r}
, and (
2.57
) for
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2),r}
. ‌We note that the independent variables in function
f^{\alpha}_{1}(\cdot)
are
v+u_{\parallel}
and
v+u_{\perp}
in the two different cases, respectively. “
du\,d\omega
” has five degrees of freedom. Therefore, when the independent variable involves
u_{\parallel}
, we can assign
u_{\parallel}
to have all three degrees of freedom while
u_{\perp}
retains two degrees of freedom. When the independent variable involves
u_{\perp}
, we must assign
u_{\perp}
to have all three degrees of freedom while
u_{\parallel}
retains two degrees of freedom.

For (
2.56
), we assign
u_{\parallel}
to have all three degrees of freedom,
u\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
,
\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
,
u_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
,
u_{\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
, and

|u_{\parallel}|^{2}(|u_{\parallel}|^{-1}d|u_{\parallel}|)d\omega=du_{\parallel},\qquad du=2(|u_{\parallel}|^{-1}d|u_{\parallel}|)du_{\perp}.

But for (
2.57
), we assign
u_{\perp}
to have all three degrees of freedom,
u\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
,
\omega^{\prime}\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
,
u_{\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
,
u_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
, and

|u_{\perp}|^{2}(|u_{\perp}|^{-1}d|u_{\perp}|)d\omega^{\prime}=du_{\perp},\qquad du=2(|u_{\perp}|^{-1}d|u_{\perp}|)du_{\parallel}.

(2.58)

We denote the three coordinates of the spherical coordinate transformation as
(\tilde{r},\tilde{\theta},\tilde{\phi})
, where
\tilde{r}\geq 0
is radial distance,
0\leq\tilde{\theta}\leq\pi
is polar angle and
0\leq\tilde{\phi}\leq 2\pi
is azimuthal angle.

In case of (
2.56
), we take
(|u_{\parallel}|,\theta,\tilde{\phi})\rightarrow(\tilde{r},\tilde{\theta},\tilde{\phi})
.

The differential area element on the unit sphere:
d\omega=\sin\theta d\theta d\tilde{\phi}
.

In case of (
2.57
), we take
(|u_{\perp}|,\varphi,\tilde{\phi})\rightarrow(\tilde{r},\tilde{\theta},\tilde{\phi})
.

The differential area element on the unit sphere:
d\omega^{\prime}=\sin\varphi d\varphi d\tilde{\phi}
.

Figure 5.
The illustration of variable substitution

From the picture (Figure 1), we have
|u_{\parallel}|=|u\cos\theta|
,
|u_{\perp}|=|u\cos\varphi|
, and
\varphi+\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}
. Therefore, it yields

du_{\perp}=|u_{\perp}|^{2}(|u_{\perp}|^{-1}d|u_{\perp}|)d\omega^{\prime}=|u_{\perp}||u_{\parallel}|(|u_{\perp}|^{-1}d|u_{\perp}|)d\omega

Together with (
2.58
), in the case of (
2.57
), we obtain

dud\omega=2|u_{\parallel}|^{-1}|u_{\perp}|^{-1}du_{\perp}du_{\parallel}

(2.59)

By (
2.26
) and (
2.29
), (
2.56
) can be written as

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{m^{\alpha}}{2}\big[|\zeta_{\parallel}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}\big]}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v+u_{\parallel})

\displaystyle\quad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{m^{\alpha}}{2}|u_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|>m}\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}\,du_{\perp}du_{\parallel}

(2.60)

where we take

\zeta_{\perp}=v_{\perp},\hskip 14.22636pt\zeta_{\parallel}=v_{\parallel}+\frac{1}{2}u_{\parallel},\hskip 14.22636ptu_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{3},\hskip 14.22636ptu_{\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}.

Then the integral kernel in (
2.32
) becomes

\displaystyle k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(1),r}(v,u_{\parallel})

\displaystyle=:\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{m^{\alpha}}{2}\big[|\zeta_{\parallel}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}\big]}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{m^{\alpha}}{2}|u_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|>m}

(2.61)

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\times\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}\,du_{\perp}.

When
\alpha=\beta
a process analogous to (
2.29
)–(
2.31
) shows that‌ ‌by combining (
2.59
), (
2.57
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\perp}|}e^{-\frac{m^{\alpha}}{2}\big[|\zeta^{\perp}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|u_{\perp}|^{2}\big]}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v+u_{\perp})

\displaystyle\quad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{m^{\alpha}}{2}|u_{\parallel}+\zeta^{\parallel}|^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|>m}\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}\,du_{\parallel}\,du_{\perp},

(2.62)

where

\zeta^{\parallel}=v_{\parallel},\hskip 14.22636pt\zeta^{\perp}=v_{\perp}+\frac{1}{2}u_{\perp},\hskip 14.22636ptu_{\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{3},\hskip 14.22636ptu_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}.

We define the integral kernel:

\displaystyle k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2),r}(v,u_{\perp})

\displaystyle=:\frac{1}{|u_{\perp}|}e^{-\frac{m^{\alpha}}{2}\big[|\zeta^{\perp}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|u_{\perp}|^{2}\big]}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{m^{\alpha}}{2}|u_{\parallel}+\zeta^{\parallel}|^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|>m}

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\times\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}\,du_{\parallel}.

(2.63)

Comparing the two expressions (
2.60
) and (
2.62
) above, we observe that they become identical if we interchange the symbols “
\parallel
” and “
\perp
” in (
2.62
). Thus, the analysis of
CaseA_{1}-CaseA_{4}
for
(
2.31
)
is directly applicable to the case of (
2.57
).

Therefore, we can state that when
m^{\alpha}=m^{\beta}
, the integral kernel
k_{\alpha\beta}^{(2),r}(v,u_{\perp},u_{\parallel})
degenerates into the integral kernel
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2),r}(v,u_{\perp})
, which is equal to
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(1),r}(v,u_{\parallel})
in the symmetric sense.

∎

According to (
2.15
) and (
2.17
),
K_{s}^{\alpha}
is expressed as

K_{s}^{\alpha}=K_{1,s}^{\alpha,1-\chi}+K_{1,r}^{\alpha,\chi}+K_{2,s}^{\alpha,1-\chi}+K_{2,r}^{\alpha,\chi}

we now present the estimates for all these parts derived thus far as follows:

By (
2.18
), (
2.19
) and (
2.22
), we obtain

\displaystyle\big\langle w^{2l}K_{1,s}^{\alpha,1-\chi}\mathbf{f}_{1},f^{\alpha}_{2}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big|_{\nu}\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big|_{\nu},

\displaystyle\big\langle w^{2l}K_{1,r}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}_{1},f^{\alpha}_{2}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq C\big[\mu^{s}(m)\big]^{\frac{1}{8}}\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big|_{\nu}\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big|_{\nu},

\displaystyle\big\langle w^{2l}K_{2,s}^{\alpha,1-\chi}\mathbf{f}_{1},f^{\alpha}_{2}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big|_{\nu}\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big|_{\nu}.

Based on all these analytical processes from (
2.23
) to (
2.63
), it can be concluded that

\left\langle w^{2l}K_{2,r}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}_{1},f^{\alpha}_{2}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\leq\frac{C_{\varepsilon}}{m}\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big|_{\nu}\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big|_{\nu}.

For any
\eta\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
we first choose sufficiently
\varepsilon
small such that

C\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}\leq\frac{\eta}{2}.

Then, for this fixed
1>\varepsilon>0
, there exists a sufficiently large
m>1
such that

C\big[\mu^{s}(m)\big]^{\frac{1}{8}}+\frac{C_{\varepsilon}}{m}\leq\frac{\eta}{2}.

Consequently, this completes the proof of (
2.16
).

Proof of Compactness
.  In the last step, we prove that
K^{\alpha}_{c}=K_{1,z}^{\alpha,\chi}+K_{2,z}^{\alpha,\chi}
is a compact operator.
Recall the expression that

\displaystyle K_{1,z}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|\leq m}b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\chi(|v-v_{*}|)\mu^{\alpha}(v)\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})}f^{\beta}(v_{*})d\omega dv_{*}

Since
|b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)|\leq C
, for fixed small
\varepsilon>0
,

\displaystyle|K_{1,z}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\{|v_{*}|\leq m\}}\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})}\big|f^{\beta}(v_{*})\big|dv_{*}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}(v)}.

Therefore, Hilbert-Schmidt theorem guarantees that
K_{1,z}^{\alpha,\chi}
is a compact operator in
L^{2}_{\nu}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
.

For the part of
K_{2,z}^{\alpha,\chi}
, we have

\displaystyle K_{2,z}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|\leq m}b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\chi(|v-v_{*}|)

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89418pt\times\Big[\mu^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}f^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})+\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}\,f^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})\Big]d\omega dv_{*}.

(2.64)

Replace ‌
\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|>m}
with ‌
\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|\leq m}
in ‌(
2.32
) and (
2.36
), the resulting expression defines the integral kernels
k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1),z}(v,u_{\parallel})
and
k_{\alpha\beta}^{(2),z}(v,u_{\perp},u_{\parallel})
.
Through analysis similar to that in the preceding discussion, we can prove that

\displaystyle-K_{2,z}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\beta=A,B}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1),z}(v,u_{\parallel})\,f^{\alpha}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\,du_{\parallel}

\displaystyle\hskip 48.36958pt+\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{2}}k_{\alpha\beta}^{(2),z}(v,u_{\perp},u_{\parallel})\,f^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\,du_{\perp}\,du_{\parallel}\Bigg).

(2.65)

For the part of
k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1),z}(v,u_{\parallel})
,

\displaystyle\big|k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1),z}(v,u_{\parallel})\big|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{2}\big[(\frac{m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}})^{2}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}\big]}\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|\leq m}.

Notice by (
2.35
) and (
2.48
), when
\alpha\neq\beta
, the part of
k_{\alpha\beta}^{(2),z}(v,u_{\perp},u_{\parallel})
satisfies

\displaystyle\big|k_{\alpha\beta}^{(2),r}(v,u_{\perp},u_{\parallel})\big|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\bar{c}(|v|^{2}+|u|^{2})}\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|\leq m}.

Since
\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}\in L^{2}_{loc}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
, by Hilbert-Schmidt theorem, we know the part of
k_{\alpha\beta}^{(2),z}(v,u_{\perp},u_{\parallel})
with
\alpha\neq\beta
in
K_{2,z}^{\alpha,\chi}
is compact.

When
\alpha=\beta
, for the part of
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2),z}(v,u_{\perp})
,

\displaystyle\big|k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2),r}(v,u_{\perp})\big|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{1}{4}(|\xi_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\eta_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\xi_{\perp}|^{2}+|\eta_{\perp}|^{2})}\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|\leq m}.

Here
u_{\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
and
\frac{1}{|u_{\perp}|}\in L^{2}_{loc}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
, by Hilbert-Schmidt theorem, this part of
K_{2,z}^{\alpha,\chi}
is also compact.

Consequently, we have completed the entire proof of Lemma
2.2
.
∎

Remark 2.1
.

Since we need to discuss the compactness of operators within the
L^{2}
framework, we thus introduce the truncation functions
\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|\geq m}
. Disregard the truncation function related to compactness. Let
K_{2}^{\alpha,\chi}=K_{2,r}^{\alpha,\chi}+K_{2,z}^{\alpha,\chi}
, which can be decomposed as follows:

K_{2}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}=\underbrace{K_{2,(1)}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}}_{\text{Typical part}}+\underbrace{K_{2,(2)}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}}_{\text{Hybrid part}},

(2.66)

where

\displaystyle K_{2,(1)}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}=\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1)}(v,u_{\parallel})\,f^{\alpha}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\,du_{\parallel},

(2.67)

\displaystyle K_{2,(2)}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{2}}k_{\alpha\beta}^{(2)}(v,u_{\perp},u_{\parallel})\,f^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\,du_{\perp}\,du_{\parallel}\quad\alpha\neq\beta

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2)}(v,u_{\perp})\,f^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp})\,du_{\perp}.

(2.68)

These integral kernels satisfy

\displaystyle k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1)}(v,u_{\parallel})

\displaystyle=:\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{2}\big[|\zeta_{\parallel}|^{2}+(\frac{m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}})^{2}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}\big]}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{2}|u_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\times\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}\,du_{\perp}.

(2.69)

If
\alpha\neq\beta
,
(
the part with exponential decay in v
)

\displaystyle k_{\alpha\beta}^{(2)}(v,u_{\perp},u_{\parallel})

\displaystyle=:\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{1}{4}(|\xi_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\eta_{\parallel}|^{2})}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\times e^{-\frac{1}{4}(|\xi_{\perp}|^{2}+|\eta_{\perp}|^{2})}\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}.

(2.70)

If
\alpha=\beta
,
(
the degenerate part
)

\displaystyle k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2)}(v,u_{\perp})

\displaystyle=:\frac{1}{|u_{\perp}|}e^{-\frac{m^{\alpha}}{2}\big[|\zeta^{\perp}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}|u_{\perp}|^{2}\big]}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-\frac{m^{\alpha}}{2}|u_{\parallel}+\zeta^{\parallel}|^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\times\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}\,du_{\parallel}.

(2.71)

In (
2.69
) and (
2.70
),
u_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{3},u_{\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
, while in (
2.71
),
u_{\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{3},u_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
. According to the analysis in Lemma
2.2
, we know (
2.70
) has exponential decay in
v
, while (
2.69
) and (
2.71
) do not. Since (
2.68
) contains integral kernels of two different types, we call it “Hybrid part”. Furthermore, based on the previous description,
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(1)}(v,u_{\parallel})
and
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2)}(v,u_{\perp})
are symmetric with respect to
u_{\parallel}
and
u_{\perp}
. Moreover,
K_{2,(2)}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}
can be also expressed by

\displaystyle K_{2,(2)}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{f}=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{2}}\hat{k}_{\alpha\beta}^{(2)}(v,u_{\parallel},u_{\perp})\,f^{\beta}(v+u_{\parallel}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\perp})\,du_{\parallel}\,du_{\perp}\quad\alpha\neq\beta

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2)}(v,u_{\perp})\,f^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp})\,du_{\perp}.

where
u_{\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{3},u_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
and

\displaystyle\hat{k}_{\alpha\beta}^{(2)}(v,u_{\parallel},u_{\perp})

\displaystyle=:\frac{1}{|u_{\perp}|}e^{-\frac{1}{4}(|\xi_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\eta_{\parallel}|^{2})}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\times e^{-\frac{1}{4}(|\xi_{\perp}|^{2}+|\eta_{\perp}|^{2})}\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}.

Due to
m^{\alpha}\neq m^{\beta}
,
u_{\parallel}
and
u_{\perp}
hold equal status, allowing us to interchange their order of integration in (
2.50
)-(
2.52
) to obtain the same result.

Remark 2.2
.

“Typical part” can be formally transformed to coincide with the Boltzmann equation for a single species of particles, and it possesses the same decay estimates
(
see (
1.6
),(
1.8
)
)
.

Let
v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel}\rightarrow v^{*}
,
s<0
, it follows that

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1)}(v,u_{\parallel})\frac{w^{s}(v)}{w^{s}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})}g^{\alpha}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})du_{\parallel}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{k}_{\alpha\beta}^{(1)}(v,v^{*})\frac{w^{s}(v)}{w^{s}(v^{*})}g^{\alpha}(v^{*})dv^{*},

which satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{w^{s}(v)}{w^{s}(v_{*})}|\tilde{k}_{\alpha\beta}^{(1)}(v,v_{*})|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\frac{(1+|v|+|v_{*}|)^{\gamma-1}}{|v-v_{*}|}\,e^{-c\{|v-v_{*}|^{2}+\frac{||v|^{2}-|v_{*}|^{2}|^{2}}{|v-v_{*}|^{2}}\}},\end{split}

(2.72)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{w^{s}(v)}{w^{s}(v_{*})}|\tilde{k}_{\alpha\beta}^{(1)}(v,v_{*})|\leq C_{\varepsilon}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma-2}.\end{split}

(2.73)

Proof.

‌Since the cases with
|u_{\parallel}|\leq 1
or
|v|\leq 1
are much simpler, we now consider the cases with
|u_{\parallel}|\geq 1
and
|v|\geq 1
. Notice by the expression (
2.32
) for
k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1)}(v,u_{\parallel})
, and (
2.30
), there exists sufficiently small
c>0
, such that

\displaystyle|k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1),r}(v,u_{\parallel})|

\displaystyle\leq:\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-2c\big[|v_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\parallel}|^{2}\big]}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-2c|u_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|>m}

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\times\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}\,du_{\perp},

and

\displaystyle e^{-c|u_{\parallel}|^{2}}\leq C\frac{w^{s}(v)}{w^{s}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})}(1+|u_{\parallel}|)^{\gamma-1}.

Combining with (
2.37
) in Case A1; (
2.40
) in Case A2; (
2.43
) in Case A3; and (
2.44
) in Case A4, it yields that

\displaystyle\frac{w^{s}(v)}{w^{s}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})}|k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1),r}(v,u_{\parallel})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\varepsilon}}{|u_{\parallel}|}(1+|u_{\parallel}|)^{\gamma-1}(1+|v|)^{\gamma-1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\varepsilon}}{|u_{\parallel}|}(1+|u_{\parallel}|+|v|)^{\gamma-1}.

(2.74)

Since
|v-v_{*}|=\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}|u_{\parallel}|
, we adjust the constant
c
to obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{w(v)}{w(v_{*})}|\tilde{k}_{\alpha\beta}^{(1)}(v,v_{*})|&=\frac{w(v)}{w(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})}|k_{\alpha\beta}^{(1)}(v,u_{\parallel})|\\
&\leq C_{\varepsilon}\frac{(1+|v|+|v_{*}-v|)^{\gamma-1}}{|v-v_{*}|}\,e^{-c\{|v-v_{*}|^{2}+|v_{\parallel}|^{2}\}}.\end{split}

(2.75)

Since

v+v_{*}=2v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel},\qquad(|v_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\parallel}|^{2})\thicksim(|2v_{\parallel}+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\parallel}|^{2}),

the exponential in (
2.75
) can be replaced by

e^{-c\{|v-v_{*}|^{2}+|2v_{\parallel}+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel}|^{2}\}}.

Then, we further write
2v_{\parallel}+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel}
as

\big|2v_{\parallel}+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel}\big|=\frac{\big|\left\langle 2v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel},\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel}\right\rangle_{v}\big|}{|\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel}|}=\frac{|\left\langle v+v_{*},v-v_{*}\right\rangle_{v}|}{|v-v_{*}|}=\frac{||v|^{2}-|v_{*}|^{2}|}{|v-v_{*}|}.

Therefore,

\big(|v-v_{*}|^{2}+|v_{\parallel}|^{2}\big)\thicksim\big(|v-v_{*}|^{2}+\frac{||v|^{2}-|v_{*}|^{2}|^{2}}{|v-v_{*}|^{2}}\big).

Combine with

(1+|v|+|v_{*}-v|)\thicksim(1+|v|+|v_{*}|),

then, (
2.72
) follows.

Notice the inequality that
|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|v_{\parallel}|^{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}(|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\parallel}|\cdot|v_{\parallel}|)
, by (
2.74
), it yields that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(1)}(v,u_{\parallel})|\frac{w(v)}{w(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})}du_{\parallel}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C_{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(1+|v|+|u_{\parallel}|)^{\gamma-1}}{|u_{\parallel}|}\,e^{-c\{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\parallel}|\cdot|v_{\parallel}|\}}du_{\parallel}.

Since
|u_{\parallel}|\cdot|v_{\parallel}|=|v\cdot u_{\parallel}|
, and
u_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we decompose
u_{\parallel}
by

u_{\parallel}=u^{0}_{\parallel}+u^{1}_{\parallel},\qquad u^{0}_{\parallel}\parallel v,\qquad u^{1}_{\parallel}\perp v,

where
u^{0}_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{1}
,
u^{1}_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
. So, it yields that

\displaystyle C_{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{1}}\frac{(1+|v|+|u_{\parallel}|)^{\gamma-1}}{|u_{\parallel}|}\,e^{-c\{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\parallel}|\cdot|v_{\parallel}|\}}du_{\parallel}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C_{\varepsilon}(1+|v|)^{\gamma-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{1}}\,e^{-c|v\cdot u_{\parallel}|}du^{0}_{\parallel}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{1}_{\parallel}|}e^{-c|u^{1}_{\parallel}|^{2}}du^{1}_{\parallel}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C_{\varepsilon}(1+|v|)^{\gamma-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{1}}\,e^{-c\{|v|u^{0}_{\parallel}\}}du^{0}_{\parallel}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C_{\varepsilon}(1+|v|)^{\gamma-2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{1}}\,e^{-c\{|v|u^{0}_{\parallel}\}}d\{|v|u^{0}_{\parallel}\}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C_{\varepsilon}(1+|v|)^{\gamma-2}.

(2.76)

This completes the proof of remark
2.2
.
∎

The integral kernels
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2)}
and
k_{\alpha\beta}^{(2)}
likewise satisfy (
2.72
), (
2.73
), since
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(2)}
is symmetric with respect to
k_{\alpha\alpha}^{(1)}
, and
k_{\alpha\beta}^{(2)}
possesses exponential decay in
v
, clearly satisfying these decay estimates. Consequently, the vector-valued integral kernel
\bm{k}(v,v_{*})
satisfies estimates (
2.72
) and (
2.73
).

Lemma 2.3
.

There exists a
\delta>0
, such that

\left\langle\mathbf{L}\mathbf{f},\mathbf{f}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}\geq\delta|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{0})\mathbf{f}|^{2}_{\nu}

(2.77)

Proof.

Assume the conclusion does not hold, then there exists a sequence of vector functions
\mathbf{f}_{n}
satisfying

\left\langle\mathbf{f}_{n},\bm{\chi}_{j}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}=0,\quad n=1,2,\cdots,\quad j=1,2,\cdots 6.

(2.78)

such that
\left\langle\mathbf{L}\mathbf{f}_{n},\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{n}
, and

\left\langle(\bm{\nu}:\mathbf{f}_{n}),\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}=|\mathbf{f}_{n}|^{2}_{\nu}\equiv 1.

Then, there exists a subsequence of
\mathbf{f}_{n}

that converges weakly to
\mathbf{f}_{0}
with respect to the inner product
\left\langle\cdot,\cdot\right\rangle_{\nu}
. The weak limit
\mathbf{f}_{0}
satisfies

|\mathbf{f}_{0}|^{2}_{\nu}\leq 1.

Note that

\left\langle\mathbf{L}\mathbf{f}_{n},\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}=\left\langle(\bm{\nu}:\mathbf{f}_{n}),\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}-\left\langle\mathbf{K}\mathbf{f}_{n},\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}=1-\left\langle\mathbf{K}\mathbf{f}_{n},\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}.

By choosing
\varepsilon
small, we split
\mathbf{K}=\mathbf{K}_{s}+\mathbf{K}_{c}
, where
\mathbf{K}_{s}
satisfies

|\left\langle\mathbf{K}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{n},\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}|\leq\varepsilon.

The compactness of
\mathbf{K}_{c}
for
\gamma\leq 0
implies

\lim_{n\to\infty}|\mathbf{K}_{c}\mathbf{f}_{n}-\mathbf{K}_{c}\mathbf{f}_{0}|_{L_{v}^{2}}=0,

which gives

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\langle\mathbf{K}_{c}\mathbf{f}_{n},\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}=\left\langle\mathbf{K}_{c}\mathbf{f}_{0},\mathbf{f}_{0}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}.

Since
\varepsilon
can be choosen arbitrarily, one gets

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\langle\mathbf{K}\mathbf{f}_{n},\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}=\left\langle\mathbf{K}\mathbf{f}_{0},\mathbf{f}_{0}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}.

We take limit
n\rightarrow\infty
to obtain

\left\langle\mathbf{L}\mathbf{f}_{0},\mathbf{f}_{0}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}=\left\langle(\bm{\nu}:\mathbf{f}_{0}),\mathbf{f}_{0}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}-\left\langle\mathbf{K}\mathbf{f}_{0},\mathbf{f}_{0}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}\leq 1-\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\langle\mathbf{K}\mathbf{f}_{n},\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}=\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\langle\mathbf{L}\mathbf{f}_{n},\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}=0.

But
\mathbf{L}
is a non-negative operator.
Therefore,
\mathbf{f}_{0}=\mathbf{P}_{0}\mathbf{f}_{0}
, together with (
2.78
), we get
\mathbf{f}_{0}=\mathbf{0}
.

It contradicts
|\mathbf{f}_{0}|^{2}_{\nu}=1
.
∎

The following lemma is used to treat the high order derivatives.

Lemma 2.4
.

Let
\tilde{\beta}
be a multi-index and
l\geq 0
. For any small positive
\eta
, there exists a constant
C_{\eta}>0
such that

\displaystyle\big\langle w^{2l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}[(\bm{\nu}:\mathbf{f})],\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big|^{2}_{\nu}-\eta\sum_{|\tilde{\beta_{1}}|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}\big|^{2}_{\nu}-C_{\eta}\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}\big|^{2}_{\nu};

(2.79)

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle w^{2l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}[\mathbf{K}\mathbf{f}_{1}],\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\eta\sum_{|\tilde{\beta_{1}}|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{1}|_{\nu}+C_{\eta}|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{1}|_{\nu}\Bigg)\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big|_{\nu}.

(2.80)

Proof.

By the chain rule of differentiation,

\big\langle w^{2l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}[(\bm{\nu}:\mathbf{f})],\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}=\big\langle w^{2l}[(\bm{\nu}:\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f})],\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}+C_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\big\langle w^{2l}[(\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\bm{\nu}:\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\mathbf{f})],\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}

(2.81)

where
\tilde{\beta}\geq\tilde{\beta_{1}}
. Moreover, a direct calculation shows that

|\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\bm{\nu}|\leq C(1+|v|)^{\gamma-1}.

(2.82)

Then we obtain

\displaystyle\big\langle w^{2l}[(\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\bm{\nu}:\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\mathbf{f})],\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}=

\displaystyle\big\langle w^{2l}[(\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\bm{\nu}:\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\mathbf{f})],\mathbf{1}_{\{|v|>m\}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\big\langle w^{2l}[(\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\bm{\nu}:\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\mathbf{f})],\mathbf{1}_{\{|v|\leq m\}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}.

By (
2.82
), the part of
|v|\geq m
is bounded by

\displaystyle\big\langle w^{2l}[(\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\bm{\nu}:\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\mathbf{f})],\mathbf{1}_{\{|v|>m\}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{m}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\mathbf{f}\big|_{\nu}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta}{2}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\mathbf{f}\big|_{\nu}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big|_{\nu}.

For the
m
chosen above, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality as well as the compact interpolation in the Sobolev space, the part of
|v|\leq m
is bounded by

\displaystyle\big\langle w^{2l}[(\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\bm{\nu}:\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\mathbf{f})],\mathbf{1}_{\{|v|\leq m\}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}\leq\frac{\eta}{2}\sum_{|\tilde{\beta_{1}}|=|\tilde{\beta}|}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\mathbf{f}\big|_{\nu}^{2}+C_{\eta}\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}\big|_{\nu}^{2}.

Therefore, this concludes (
2.79
).

Next we turn to the estimate for (
2.80
). From the decomposition
\mathbf{K}=\mathbf{K}_{1}+\mathbf{K}_{2}
, first we consider
\big\langle w^{2l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{K}_{1}\mathbf{f}_{1},\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}
, which is bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}C_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\iiint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}w^{2l}(v)|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}(v)}\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}+v)}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}f^{\beta}_{1}(v_{*}+v)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}f^{\alpha}_{2}(v)b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,d\omega\,dv_{*}\,dv

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}C_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\Bigg(\iiint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}w^{2l}(v)|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}(v)}\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}+v)}\big)

(2.83)

\displaystyle\hskip 156.49014pt\times\chi(|v_{*}|)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}f^{\beta}_{1}(v_{*}+v)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}f^{\alpha}_{2}(v)b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,d\omega\,dv_{*}\,dv

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\iiint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}w^{2l}(v)|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}(v)}\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}+v)}\big)

(2.84)

\displaystyle\hskip 147.95424pt\times\big[1-\chi(|v_{*}|)\big]\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}f^{\beta}_{1}(v_{*}+v)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}f^{\alpha}_{2}(v)b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,d\omega\,dv_{*}\,dv\Bigg),

where we have changed variable
v_{*}-v\rightarrow v_{*}
. Note that
\cos\theta=\frac{\omega\cdot(v-v_{*})}{|v-v_{*}|}
and
|b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)|\leq C
, it yields that

\Big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}(v)}\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}+v)}\big)\Big|\leq Ce^{-\frac{m_{s}}{8}(|v+v_{*}|^{2}+|v|^{2})},

where we denote
m_{s}=\min\{m^{A},m^{B}\}
.
Applying a further change of variable and integrating
\omega
over
\mathbb{S}^{2}
. (
2.84
) is bounded by

\displaystyle C\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\sum_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\iint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}e^{-\frac{m_{s}}{8}(|v_{*}|^{2}+|v|^{2})}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}w^{2l}(v)

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\big[1-\chi(|v-v_{*}|)\big]\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}f^{\beta}_{1}(v_{*})\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}f^{\alpha}_{2}(v)\big|\,dv_{*}\,dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\sum_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\frac{m_{s}|v|^{2}}{8}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}w^{2l}(v)\big[1-\chi(|v-v_{*}|)\big]dv

\displaystyle\hskip 233.3125pt\times e^{-\frac{m_{s}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}f^{\beta}_{1}(v_{*})|^{2}\,dv_{*}\,\Bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\frac{m_{s}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\big[1-\chi(|v-v_{*}|)\big]dv_{*}\,e^{-\frac{m_{s}|v|^{2}}{8}}w^{2l}(v)|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}f^{\alpha}_{2}(v)|^{2}dv\Bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}\sum_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big|_{\nu}\,\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{\eta}{2}\Big(\sum_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}\leq\tilde{\beta}}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big|_{\nu}\Big)\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big|_{\nu}

For the small
\varepsilon>0
chosen above, since
\chi_{\varepsilon}
vanishes near the origin, an integration over
v_{*}
in (
2.83
) yields

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}C_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\iiint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}^{v_{*}}\Big[|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\chi(|v_{*}|)\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\big(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}(v)}\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}+v)}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 184.9429pt\times w^{2l}(v)f^{\beta}_{1}(v_{*}+v)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}f^{\alpha}_{2}(v)b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,d\omega\,dv_{*}\,dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}C_{\varepsilon}\iint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}e^{-\frac{m_{s}}{8}(|v+v_{*}|^{2}+|v|^{2})}|f^{\beta}_{1}(v_{*}+v)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}f^{\alpha}_{2}(v)|dv_{*}\,dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\,C_{\varepsilon}\,\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big|_{\nu}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big|_{\nu}

where we have used notation
\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}^{v_{*}}
to denote the derivative with respect to
v_{*}
, in order to distinguish it from notation
\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta_{1}}}
which represents the derivative with respect to
v
. Therefore, the estimate for the part of
\mathbf{K}_{1}
can be derived.

Now we consider the part for
\mathbf{K}_{2}
.
\mathbf{K}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{1}
takes the form

\displaystyle\sum_{\beta=A,B}

\displaystyle\Bigg(\iint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|u|^{\gamma}[\mu^{\beta}(u+v)]^{\frac{1}{2}}\big[1-\chi(|u|)\big]\big[\mu^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}

(2.85)

\displaystyle\hskip 156.49014pt\times f^{\alpha}_{1}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,du\,d\omega

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt+\iint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|u|^{\gamma}[\mu^{\beta}(u+v)]^{\frac{1}{2}}\big[1-\chi(|u|)\big]\big[\mu^{\alpha}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}

(2.86)

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\times f^{\beta}_{1}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,du\,d\omega

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt+\iint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|u|^{\gamma}[\mu^{\beta}(u+v)]^{\frac{1}{2}}\chi(|u|)\big[\mu^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}

(2.87)

\displaystyle\hskip 156.49014pt\times f^{\alpha}_{1}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,du\,d\omega

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt+\iint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|u|^{\gamma}[\mu^{\beta}(u+v)]^{\frac{1}{2}}\chi(|u|)\big[\mu^{\alpha}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}

(2.88)

\displaystyle\hskip 150.79968pt\times f^{\beta}_{1}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,du\,d\omega\Bigg).

To compute
\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{K}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{1}
, differentiate the second term above directly with respect to
\beta
using the product rule. By a further change of variables, this approach bypasses the need to compute derivatives of the singular kernel
|u-v|^{\gamma}
in (
2.85
) and (
2.86
).
Consequently, the term
\langle w^{2l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{K}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{1},\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\rangle
can be decomposed as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\sum_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}C_{\tilde{\beta}}\Bigg(\iiint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}w^{2l}(v)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}[\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})]^{\frac{1}{2}}\big[1-\chi(|v-v_{*}|)\big]\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}[\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 233.3125pt\times\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{3}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}f^{\alpha}_{2}(v)b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,du\,dv\,d\omega

(2.89)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt+\iiint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}w^{2l}(v)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}[\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})]^{\frac{1}{2}}\big[1-\chi(|v-v_{*}|)\big]\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}[\mu^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 256.0748pt\times\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{3}}f^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}f^{\alpha}_{2}(v)b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,dv_{*}\,dv\,d\omega\Bigg)

(2.90)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt+\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\Big[\iint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}[\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})]^{\frac{1}{2}}\chi(|v-v_{*}|)[\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})]^{\frac{1}{2}}f^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,du\,d\omega\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 298.75394pt\times w^{2l}(v)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}f^{\alpha}_{2}(v)\,dv

(2.91)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\Big(\iint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}[\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})]^{\frac{1}{2}}\chi(|v-v_{*}|)[\mu^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})]^{\frac{1}{2}}f^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,du\,d\omega\Big)w^{2l}(v)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}f^{\alpha}_{2}(v)\,dv\Bigg),

(2.92)

where
\tilde{\beta}=\tilde{\beta}_{1}+\tilde{\beta}_{2}+\tilde{\beta}_{3}
. Since

\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}[\mu^{\beta}(\cdot)]^{\frac{1}{2}}\big|\leq Ce^{-\frac{m_{s}|\cdot|^{2}}{8}}.

Following a similar procedure as in (
2.20
) to (
2.22
), the terms (
2.89
) and (
2.90
) are bounded by

\varepsilon^{3+\gamma}\sum_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{1}|_{\nu}\,|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}|_{\nu}\leq\frac{\eta}{2}\Big(\sum_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}\leq\tilde{\beta}}|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{1}|_{\nu}\Big)|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}|_{\nu}.

For the small
\varepsilon>0
above, (
2.91
), (
2.92
)can be written as

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}

\displaystyle C_{\tilde{\beta}}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\big(e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{2}|\zeta_{\parallel}|^{2}}\big)e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{2}(\frac{m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}})^{2}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}f^{\alpha}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})

(2.93)

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\big(e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{2}|u_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}\,du_{\perp}du_{\parallel}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{1}{4}[|\xi_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\eta_{\parallel}|^{2}]}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)

(2.94)

\displaystyle\hskip 36.98866pt\times e^{-\frac{1}{4}(|\xi_{\perp}|^{2}+|\eta_{\perp}|^{2})}\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}f^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\,du_{\perp}du_{\parallel}\Bigg).

By the expression of
\eta_{\parallel},\xi_{\perp},\eta_{\perp},\xi_{\parallel}
and
\zeta_{\perp},\zeta_{\parallel}
, we deduce that

|\nabla_{v}\eta_{\parallel}|+|\nabla_{v}\eta_{\perp}|+|\nabla_{v}\xi_{\parallel}|+|\nabla_{v}\xi_{\perp}|+|\nabla_{v}\zeta_{\parallel}|+|\nabla_{v}\zeta_{\perp}|\leq C,

which further gives

\displaystyle\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}\big(e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{2}|\zeta_{\parallel}|^{2}}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{4}|\zeta_{\parallel}|^{2}},

\displaystyle\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}\big(e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{2}|u_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{4}|u_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}},

\displaystyle\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\big(e^{-\frac{1}{4}(|\xi_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\eta_{\parallel}|^{2})}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{-\frac{1}{8}(|\xi_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\eta_{\parallel}|^{2})},

\displaystyle\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\big(e^{-\frac{1}{4}(|\xi_{\perp}|^{2}+|\eta_{\perp}|^{2})}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{-\frac{1}{8}(|\xi_{\perp}|^{2}+|\eta_{\perp}|^{2})}.

Consequently, apart from the factors in the exponents, equation (
2.93
),(
2.94
) is almost identical to (
2.26
),(
2.27
). By choosing
m>0
large enough, the integration domain is further partitioned into
(|v|+|v_{*}|\leq m)
and
(|v|+|v_{*}|\geq m)
.

Applying analogous estimation techniques for
\mathbf{K}_{2,r}^{\chi}\mathbf{f}_{1}
as demonstrated in (
2.37
)–(
2.45
) and (
2.49
)–(
2.55
), the contribution from the region
(|v|+|v_{*}|\geq m)
can be bounded by

\frac{C_{\varepsilon}}{m}\Bigg(\sum_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big|_{\nu}\Bigg)\,\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big|_{\nu}\leq\frac{\eta}{2}\Bigg(\sum_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}\leq\tilde{\beta}}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big|_{\nu}\Bigg)\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big|_{\nu}.

Finally, we consider the remaining part, the operator
\mathbf{K}_{2,z}^{\chi}

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\sum_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}C_{\tilde{\beta}}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\big(e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{2}|\zeta_{\parallel}|^{2}}\big)e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{2}(\frac{m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}})^{2}|u_{\parallel}|^{2}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}f^{\alpha}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\big(e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}}{2}|u_{\perp}+\zeta_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|\leq m}\,du_{\perp}du_{\parallel}

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}e^{-\frac{1}{4}(|\xi_{\parallel}|^{2}+|\eta_{\parallel}|^{2})}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)}{|\cos\theta|}\chi\big(\sqrt{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\times e^{-\frac{1}{4}(|\xi_{\perp}|^{2}+|\eta_{\perp}|^{2})}\left[|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}\right]^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}f^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})\mathbf{1}_{|v|+|v_{*}|\leq m}\,du_{\perp}du_{\parallel}\Bigg)

is compact since
\frac{1}{|u_{\parallel}|}\in L^{2}_{loc}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
. Hence, there exists a
C_{\eta}>0
such that
\left\langle w^{2l}\mathbf{K}_{2,r}^{\chi}\mathbf{f}_{1},\mathbf{f}_{2}\right\rangle_{\nu}
is bounded by

\displaystyle\Bigg(\frac{\eta}{4}\sum_{|\tilde{\beta_{1}}|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta_{1}}}\mathbf{f}\big|_{\nu}^{2}+C_{\eta}\big|w^{l}\mathbf{f}\big|_{\nu}^{2}\Bigg)\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big|_{\nu}.

Based on the above analysis, we have completed the proof of this lemma.
∎

3.
The nonlinear collision operator

This section is primarily devoted to discussing derivative estimates in both space and velocity for the nonlinear collision operator
\bf{\Gamma}\big[f_{1},f_{2}\big]
.

\displaystyle\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}\big[f^{\alpha}_{1},f^{\beta}_{2}\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt=\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{S^{2}}|u|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|u+v|^{2}}{4}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v+\frac{2m^{\beta}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})

\displaystyle\hskip 136.5733pt\times f^{\beta}_{2}(v+u_{\perp}+\frac{m^{\beta}-m^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}+m^{\beta}}u_{\parallel})b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,du\,d\omega\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 54.06006pt-\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{S^{2}}|u|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|u+v|^{2}}{4}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v)f^{\beta}_{2}(v+u)b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,du\,d\omega\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt=\sum_{\tilde{\beta}=\tilde{\beta}_{0}+\tilde{\beta}_{1}+\tilde{\beta}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{\alpha}=\tilde{\alpha}_{1}+\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}C_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\beta}_{0}\tilde{\beta}_{1}\tilde{\beta}_{2}}C_{\tilde{\alpha}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{0}\big[\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big]

where we have changed a variable
v_{*}-v\rightarrow u
. Subsequently, by the product rule and an inverse substitution of variables,

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{0}\big[\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{S^{2}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{0}}\big(e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\big)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,dv_{*}\,d\omega

\displaystyle\hskip 45.52458pt-\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{S^{2}}|u-v|^{\gamma}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{0}}\big(e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\big)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,dv_{*}\,d\omega

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt=:\Gamma_{0,\text{gain}}^{\alpha\beta}-\Gamma_{0,\text{loss}}^{\alpha\beta}.

(3.1)

Lemma 3.1
.

Let
-3<\gamma<0
,
N\geq 8
. The multiple indicators
\tilde{\beta}_{0},\tilde{\beta}_{1},\tilde{\beta}_{2}
and
\tilde{\alpha}_{1},\tilde{\alpha}_{2}
satisfy
\tilde{\beta}_{0}+\tilde{\beta}_{1}+\tilde{\beta}_{2}=\beta
and
\tilde{\alpha}_{1}+\tilde{\alpha}_{2}=\tilde{\alpha}
. Suppose that
|\tilde{\beta}|\leq l
. If
|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|\geq\frac{N}{2}
, then there exists some constant
C
such that

\displaystyle\Big|\left\langle w^{2l}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{0}[\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}],\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{3}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Bigg)\big\|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big\|_{\nu}\big\|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{3}\big\|_{\nu}

(3.2)

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91684pt+C\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{2}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Bigg)\big\|w^{l-|\tilde{\beta}_{2}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big\|_{\nu}\big\|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{3}\big\|_{\nu}.

Moreover, if
|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|\leq\frac{N}{2}
, then

\displaystyle\Big|\left\langle w^{2l}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{0}[\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}],\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{3}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Bigg)\big\|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big\|_{\nu}\big\|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{3}\big\|_{\nu}

(3.3)

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91684pt+C\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Bigg)\big\|w^{l-|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big\|_{\nu}\big\|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{3}\big\|_{\nu}.

Proof.

First we consider the loss term
\Gamma_{0,\text{loss}}^{\alpha\beta}
. Since
\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{0}}\big(e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\big)\leq Ce^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}
, then we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{0}}\big(e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\big)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})\big|\,dv_{*}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})\big|^{2}dv_{*}\Big)^{1/2}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}dv_{*}\Big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\sup_{x,v_{*}}\Big|e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})\Big|\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}dv_{*}\Big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\sup_{x,v_{*}}\Big|e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})\Big|\times\big(1+|v|\big)^{\gamma}

Notice that
|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|+|\tilde{\alpha}_{2}|\leq\frac{N}{2}
,
N\geq 8
, the Sobolev embedding theorem implies
H^{4}(T^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\subset L^{\infty}
, then

\sup_{x,v_{*}}\Big|e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})\Big|\leq C\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

Since
b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\leq C
, it follows that

\displaystyle\Big|\left\langle w^{2l}\Gamma_{0,\text{loss}}^{\alpha\beta}[\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}],\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{3}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}(1+|v|)^{\gamma}w^{2l}(v)\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(x,v)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{3}(x,v)\big|dv\,dx

\displaystyle\hskip 179.25244pt\times\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Bigg)\big\|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big\|_{\nu}\big\|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{3}\big\|_{\nu}

The case of
|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|+|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq\frac{N}{2}
is thereby proven for
\Gamma_{0,\text{loss}}^{\alpha\beta}
.

Next we turn to the case of
|\tilde{\alpha}_{2}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{2}|\leq\frac{N}{2}
, and split the integration domain into three parts

\displaystyle D_{1}=\big(|v_{*}-v|\leq\frac{|v|}{2}\big),\hskip 34.14322ptD_{2}=\Big[\big(|v_{*}-v|\geq\frac{|v|}{2}\big)\cap\big(|v|\geq 1\big)\Big],

\displaystyle D_{3}=\Big[\big(|v_{*}-v|\geq\frac{|v|}{2}\big)\cap\big(|v|\leq 1\big)\Big].

For the first part
(v_{*},v)\in D_{1}
,
|v_{*}|\geq|v|-|v_{*}-v|\geq\frac{|v|}{2}
, which gives

e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}\leq e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{16}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v|^{2}}{64}}.

Then,
\left\langle w^{2l}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{0}\big[\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big],\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{3}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}
in
D_{1}
can be bounded by

\displaystyle C\iiint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|v_{*}-v\right|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{16}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v|^{2}}{64}}w^{2l}(v)

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\times\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(x,v)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{3}(x,v)\big|\,dv_{*}\,dv\,dx

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07182pt\leq C\Bigg(\iiint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|v_{*}-v\right|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{16}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v|^{2}}{64}}w^{2l}(v)\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})\big|^{2}\,dv_{*}\,dv\,dx\Bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\Bigg(\iiint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|v_{*}-v\right|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{16}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v|^{2}}{64}}w^{2l}(v)

\displaystyle\hskip 179.25244pt\times\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(x,v)\big|^{2}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{3}(x,v)\big|^{2}\,dv_{*}\,dv\,dx\Bigg)^{1/2}.

‌Integration over
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}

in the first factor establishes an upper bound

\displaystyle C\Bigg(\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\Big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v|^{2}}{32}}dv_{*}\Big]e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v|^{2}}{64}}w^{2l}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(x,v)\big|^{2}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{3}(x,v)\big|^{2}dvdx\Bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 284.52756pt\times\big\|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big\|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\hskip 284.52756pt\times\big\|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big\|_{\nu}

Once again applying the Sobolev embedding theorem,

.

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt\leq C\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})\big|du\right)\left(\int_{|v|\geq 1}|v|^{\gamma}w^{2l}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(x,v)\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{3}(x,v)\big|dv\right)dx

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})|du\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\Bigg)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}^{\prime}-v^{\prime}|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}{128}}dv_{*}^{\prime}

from

\displaystyle\hskip 105.2751pt\quad\times\Bigg(\int|v|^{\gamma}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})\big|^{2}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})\big|^{2}dv_{*}\Bigg)^{1/2}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{3}(v)\big|dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt\leq C\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left(\int_{|v|\leq 1,|v_{*}|\leq\frac{1}{2}}|v|^{\gamma}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})\big|^{2}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})\big|^{2}dv_{*}\right)^{1/2}\left(\int_{|v|\leq 1}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{3}(v)\big|^{2}dv\right)^{1/2}dx.

(63)

But from (
3.4
),

\displaystyle\int_{|v|\leq 1,|v_{*}|\leq\frac{1}{2}}|v|^{\gamma}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})\big|^{2}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})\big|^{2}dv_{*}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C,|v_{*}^{\prime}|\leq C}\min\big\{|v^{\prime}|^{\gamma},|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\big\}\times\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})\big|^{2}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})\big|^{2}dv_{*}^{\prime}dv^{\prime}.

Assuming

, the above is bounded by

\displaystyle C\Big(\int_{|v_{*}^{\prime}|\leq C}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{\gamma}|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}dv_{*}^{\prime}\Big)\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})\big|^{2}dv^{\prime}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\Big(\sup_{|v_{*}^{\prime}|\leq C}\big|\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})\big|^{2}\Big)\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})\big|^{2}_{\nu}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{2}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\mathbf{f}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\Bigg)\big|w^{l}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{1}\big|^{2}_{\nu}

Through a further integration over
x
, then this lemma can be concluded.
∎

Lemma 3.2
.

Let
|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|+|\tilde{\alpha}_{2}|=N
,
N\geq 8
.

(1)

Let
\chi_{1}(x,v)
be a smooth function with compact support, then

\displaystyle\left\|\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big]\chi_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 51.21504pt\leq C\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\right\|_{\nu},\hskip 11.38092pt\text{if}\ \left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\geq\frac{N}{2};

(3.5)

\displaystyle\left\|\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big]\chi_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 51.21504pt\leq C\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\right\|_{\nu},\hskip 11.38092pt\text{if}\ \left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\leq\frac{N}{2}.

(3.6)

(2)

Let
\bar{\chi}_{1}(v)
be a smooth function such that

\sup\left\{\left|\bar{\chi}_{1}\right|+\left|\nabla\bar{\chi}_{1}\right|+\left|\nabla^{2}\bar{\chi}_{1}\right|\right\}\leq C\min\{\mu^{A},\mu^{B}\},

then it holds that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\bar{\chi}_{1}dv\right|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\hskip 51.21504pt\leq C\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\right\|_{\nu},\hskip 11.38092pt\text{if}\ \left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\geq\frac{N}{2};

(3.7)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\bar{\chi}_{1}dv\right|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\hskip 51.21504pt\leq C\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\right\|_{\nu},\hskip 11.38092pt\text{if}\ \left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\leq\frac{N}{2}.

(3.8)

Proof.

We note that

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]

\displaystyle\equiv\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{S^{2}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,dv_{*}\,d\omega

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{S^{2}}|u-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,dv_{*}\,d\omega

\displaystyle=:\Gamma_{\text{gain}}^{\alpha\beta}-\Gamma_{\text{loss}}^{\alpha\beta}.

In order to obtain (
3.5
) and (
3.6
), we first estimate the second loss term
\Gamma_{\text{loss}}^{\alpha\beta}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big]
.

If
|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\geq\frac{N}{2}
, by
H^{4}(\mathbf{T}^{3}\times\mathbf{R}^{3})\subset L^{\infty}
and
N\geq 8
,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})\big|dv_{*}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\sup_{x,v_{*}}\left|e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})\right|\big(1+|v|\big)^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\big(1+|v|\big)^{\gamma}.

Therefore,
\left\|\Gamma_{\text{loss}}^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\chi_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}
is bounded by

\displaystyle\left(\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\Gamma_{\text{loss}}^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\chi_{1}\right|^{2}dvdx\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}(1+|v|)^{\gamma}\chi_{1}^{2}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}|^{2}dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\right\|_{\nu}.

This concludes the case of
|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\geq\frac{N}{2}
. If
|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq\frac{N}{2}
, then the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality leads to

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})|dv_{*}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})|^{2}dv_{*}\right)^{1/2}(1+|v|)^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}.

Since
\gamma>-3
, and
\chi_{1}
has compact support,

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}(1+|v|)^{\gamma}\chi_{1}dv<\infty.

Then we get

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\Gamma_{\text{loss}}^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\chi_{1}\right|^{2}dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\iiint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(x,v)|^{2}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})|^{2}(1+|v|)^{\gamma}\chi_{1}^{2}dv_{*}dvdx,

and it can be further bounded by

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*})|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 22.76228pt\times\sup_{x,v}\Big\{|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(x,v)|^{2}\chi_{1}(x,v)\Big\}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}(1+|v|)^{\gamma}\chi_{1}dv\right)dv_{*}dx

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

The next step is to estimate the gain term
\Gamma_{\text{gain}}^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]
in (
3.5
) and (
3.6
). We notice the condition that
\chi_{1}(x,v)
has a compact support, so one gets
\chi_{1}(x,v)\leq C\min\{\mu^{A},\mu^{B}\}
, and then

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\Gamma_{\text{gain}}^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\chi_{1}\right|^{2}dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\iiint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}|u-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(x,v^{\prime})|^{2}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}\chi_{1}^{2}(v)dv_{*}dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt\times\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}dv_{*}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\iiint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}|v_{*}^{\prime}-v^{\prime}|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(x,v^{\prime})|^{2}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}dv_{*}^{\prime}dv^{\prime}dx.

(3.9)

Based on the symmetry between
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}
and
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}
, without loss of generality, we assume
\left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\geq\frac{N}{2}
, so it yields that

\sup_{x,v_{*}^{\prime}}\Big\{e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}\Big\}\leq C\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Together with the fact
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}^{\prime}-v^{\prime}|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}dv_{*}^{\prime}<\infty
,
\gamma>-3
, we can bound (
3.9
) by

\displaystyle\iint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}\left(\int|v_{*}^{\prime}-v^{\prime}|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(x,v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}dv_{*}^{\prime}\right)e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(x,v^{\prime})|^{2}dv^{\prime}dx

(3.10)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)^{2}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\big\|^{2}_{\nu}.

This completes the proof of (
3.5
) and (
3.6
).

In order to verify (
3.7
) and (
3.8
), since
\bar{\chi}_{1}(v)
exhibits exponential decay,
\Gamma_{\text{loss}}^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]
can be estimated by

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Gamma_{\text{loss}}^{\alpha\beta}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big]\bar{\chi}_{1}(v)dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\bar{\chi}_{1}(v)\big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})\big|dv_{*}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\bar{\chi}_{1}(v)\big|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})\big|^{2}dv_{*}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 159.3356pt\times\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}dv_{*}\right)^{1/2}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\left(\iint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v)\big|^{2}\big|\bar{\chi}_{1}(v)\big|\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})\big|^{2}dv_{*}dv\right)^{1/2},

where we have used the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality for the
v
integration. We notice the symmetry between
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}
and
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}
. Without loss of generality, we assume
\left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\geq\frac{N}{2}
, which gives

\sup_{x,v_{*}}\left\{e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})|^{2}\right\}\leq C\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

Since
0>\gamma>-3
, we first integrate over
u
, and then take the
L^{2}
norm in the spatial variable
x
above concludes (
3.7
) and (
3.8
) for the loss term.

To prove (
3.7
) and (
3.8
) for the gain term, since

\sup_{v}\left\{\left|\bar{\chi}_{1}\right|+\left|\nabla\bar{\chi}_{1}\right|+\left|\nabla^{2}\bar{\chi}_{1}\right|\right\}\leq C\min\big\{\mu^{A},\mu^{B}\big\},

we obtain
