\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Gamma_{\text{gain}}^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\bar{\chi}_{1}(v)dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\iint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{32}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})\bar{\chi}_{1}(v)|\,dv_{*}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{32}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})|^{2}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}\,dv_{*}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 150.79968pt\times\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{32}}\,dv_{*}\right)^{1/2}|\bar{\chi}_{1}(v)|\,dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\left(\iint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{32}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})|^{2}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}|\chi_{1}|\,dv_{*}dv\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\left(\iint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}|v_{*}^{\prime}-v^{\prime}|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v^{\prime}|^{2}}{32}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}{32}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})|^{2}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}\,dv_{*}^{\prime}dv^{\prime}\right)^{1/2},

where we have applied the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality for
v_{*}
. By a similar argument as (
3.10
), it is bounded by

C\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\big|_{\nu}.

By a further integration in the spatial variable
x
, we can conclude (
3.7
). We notice the symmetry between
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}
and
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}
, the proof of case
\left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\leq\frac{N}{2}
will be the same. Therefore, we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

Lemma 3.3
.

Let
|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|+|\tilde{\alpha}_{2}|=N
,
N\geq 8
, and
\chi_{1}(x,v)
be a smooth function with compact support. For any
\eta>0
, then it holds

(1)

If
\left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\geq\frac{N}{2}
, there exists
f^{\alpha}_{1,\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}(x,v_{*})
such that
\|f^{\alpha}_{1,\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\|\leq C_{\eta}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\|_{\nu}
, and

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\chi_{1}dvdx

(3.11)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}f^{\alpha}_{1,\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\,dv_{*}dx+O(\eta^{3+\gamma})\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\right\|_{\nu}.

(2)

If
\left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\leq\frac{N}{2}
, there exists
f^{\beta}_{2,\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}(x,v)
such that
\|f^{\beta}_{2,\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\|\leq C_{\eta}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\|_{\nu}
, and

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\chi_{1}dvdx

(3.12)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}f^{\beta}_{2,\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\,dvdx+O(\eta^{3+\gamma})\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big\|_{\nu}.

Proof.

To verify (
3.11
), we apply changes of variables
(v_{*},v)\to(v_{*}^{\prime},v^{\prime})\to(v_{*},v)
for the gain term to obtain

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\chi_{1}(x,v)\,dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt=\iiiint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)|v_{*}^{\prime}-v^{\prime}|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\chi_{1}(x,v)\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v^{\prime})\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*}^{\prime})d\omega dv_{*}dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\iiiint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\chi_{1}(x,v)\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v)\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})d\omega dv_{*}dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt=\iiiint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\left[e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}\chi_{1}(x,v^{\prime})-e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\chi_{1}(x,v)\right]

(3.13)

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt\times\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v)\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})d\omega dv_{*}dvdx.

If
\left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\geq\frac{N}{2}
, by the cutoff function
\chi
in (
2.10
) with
\varepsilon=\eta
, we define

\displaystyle f^{\alpha}_{1,\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}(x,v_{*})=

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\chi(|v_{*}-v|)\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v)

(3.14)

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\times\left[e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}\chi_{1}(x,v^{\prime})-e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\chi_{1}(x,v)\right]d\omega dv.

Using (
1.2
),

\left|e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}\chi_{1}(x,v^{\prime})\right|\leq Ce^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}=Ce^{-\frac{m^{\beta}(|v_{*}|^{2}+|v|^{2})}{4}}.

Since
\chi
vanishes near the origin,

|f^{\alpha}_{1,\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}(x,v_{*})|\leq C_{\eta}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}|_{\nu}.

So, it yields

\|f^{\alpha}_{1,\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\|\leq C_{\eta}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\|_{\nu}.

(3.15)

Notice by (
3.13
), (
3.14
) and (
3.15
),

\Big|\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\chi_{1}dvdx-\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}f^{\alpha}_{1,\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\,dv_{*}dx\Big|

is bounded by

\displaystyle C\iiint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\big[1-\chi(|v_{*}-v|)\big]\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v)\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})\big|e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v|^{2}}{4}}dv_{*}dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq\sup_{x,v_{*}}\big\{e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{16}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})|\big\}\int_{|v_{*}-v|\leq 2\eta}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{16}}dv_{*}

\displaystyle\hskip 207.7052pt\times\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v|^{2}}{8}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v)|dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\eta^{3+\gamma}\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\big\|_{\nu}.

Thus we have proved (
3.11
).

Next, we turn to prove (
3.12
), with
\left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\leq\frac{N}{2}
. By the same changes of variables, we define

\displaystyle f^{\beta}_{2,\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}(x,v)=

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\chi(|v_{*}-v|)\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})

(3.16)

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\times\left(e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}\chi_{1}(x,v^{\prime})-e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\chi_{1}(x,v)\right)d\omega dv_{*}.

It satisfies

|f^{\beta}_{2,\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}(x,v)|\leq C_{\eta}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}|_{\nu}.

(3.17)

By (
3.16
) and (
3.17
),

\Big|\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}]\chi_{1}dvdx-\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}f^{\beta}_{2,\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\,dvdx\Big|

is bounded by

\displaystyle C\iiint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\big[1-\chi(|v_{*}-v|)\big]\Big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v)\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})\Big|\times\Big|e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}{4}}\chi_{1}(x,v^{\prime})

\displaystyle\hskip 241.84842pt-e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{4}}\chi_{1}(x,v)\Big|dv_{*}dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}x,v\end{subarray}}\big\{e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v|^{2}}{16}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}f^{\alpha}_{1}(v)|\big\}\int_{|v_{*}-v|\geq 2\eta}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v|^{2}}{16}}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 207.7052pt\times\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{T}^{3}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{8}}|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}(v_{*})|dv_{*}dx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\eta^{3+\gamma}\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}f^{\alpha}_{1}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Bigg)\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}f^{\beta}_{2}\big\|_{\nu}.

This concludes (
3.12
). Therefore, we have proved this lemma.
∎

4.
Energy estimates

This section establishes a time-local solution for the Boltzmann equation. Our approach, therefore, relies on a uniform energy estimate for the following sequence of iterating approximate solutions.

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\right)F_{n+1}^{\alpha}+\sum_{\beta=A,B}F^{\alpha}_{n+1}\iint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}F^{\beta}_{n}(v_{*})d\omega dv_{*}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{\beta=A,B}\iint_{\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}F^{\alpha}_{n}(v^{\prime})F^{\beta}_{n}(v^{\prime}_{*})d\omega dv_{*},

(4.1)

with initial data

\mathbf{F}_{n+1}(0,x,v)=\mathbf{F}_{0}(x,v).

Since
\mathbf{F}_{n+1}=\bm{\mu}+(\sqrt{\bm{\mu}}:\mathbf{f}_{n+1})
, and
\mathbf{f}_{n+1}=(f_{n+1}^{A},f_{n+1}^{B})^{T}
satisfies

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}+\nu^{\alpha}\right)f_{n+1}^{\alpha}+K^{\alpha}\mathbf{f}_{n}=\sum_{\beta=A,B}\Big(\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{gain}}[f_{n}^{\alpha},f_{n}^{\beta}]-\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{loss}}[f^{\alpha}_{n},f^{\beta}_{n+1}]\Big),

(4.2)

\displaystyle\hskip 96.73918ptf_{n+1}^{\alpha}(0,x,v)=f_{0}^{\alpha}(x,v).

The most important thing is to get a uniform-in-
n
estimate for the energy
\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(t)]
.

Lemma 4.1
.

The sequence of approximate solutions
\{\mathbf{F}_{n}(t,x,v)\}
is non-negative. For sufficiently small
{\mathcal{E}}(\mathbf{f}_{0})
, there exists
T^{*}({\mathcal{E}}(\mathbf{f}_{0}))>0
, such that

\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq t\leq T^{*},\ 1\leq k\leq\infty\end{subarray}}{\mathcal{E}}[\mathbf{f}_{k}(t)]\leq C{\mathcal{E}}(\mathbf{f}_{0}).

(4.3)

Proof.

We prove this by induction over k. ‌It is straightforward to observe that (
4.3
) satisfies the case of
k=0
. We assume (
4.3
) holds for
k=n
. The linear equation (
4.1
) admits a solution
\mathbf{F}_{n+1}
given any
\mathbf{F}_{n}\geq 0
. Furthermore, the non-negativity of
F_{0}(x,v)
ensures
\mathbf{F}_{n+1}(t,x,v)\geq 0
.

To prove (
4.3
) for
k=n+1
, we need to estimate the mixed
x
and
v
derivatives of
f^{n+1}
. Taking
\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}
(
\tilde{\beta}\neq 0
) of (
4.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle[\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}]\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f_{n+1}^{\alpha}+\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}[\nu^{\alpha}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}f_{n+1}^{\alpha}]+\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}[K^{\alpha}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}]+C^{\tilde{\beta}}_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}v_{j}\partial^{j}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta}_{1}}f_{n+1}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\beta=A,B}\Big(\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{gain}}[f_{n}^{\alpha},f_{n}^{\beta}]-\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{loss}}[f_{n+1}^{\alpha},f_{n}^{\beta}]\Big),

where
|\tilde{\beta}|=1
. We multiply the above equation with
w^{2|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{n+1}
, integrating it over
\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
and then sum over
\alpha=A,B
to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\left\langle w^{2|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}(\nu^{\alpha}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}f_{n+1}^{\alpha}),\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f_{n+1}^{\alpha}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\left\langle w^{2|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}(\mathbf{K}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}),\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\left\langle w^{2|\tilde{\beta}|}C^{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}(\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}v_{j})\partial^{j}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1},\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\left\langle w^{2|\tilde{\beta}|}\Big(\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{gain}}[f_{n}^{\alpha},f_{n}^{\beta}]-\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{loss}}[f_{n+1}^{\alpha},f_{n}^{\beta}]\Big),\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{n+1}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(4.4)

‌Next, we estimate the terms in (
4.4
) term by term. For any
\eta>0
, using Lemma
2.4
, then integrating over
\mathbb{T}^{3}
yields

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\left\langle w^{2|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}(\nu^{\alpha}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}f_{n+1}^{\alpha}),\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f_{n+1}^{\alpha}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\geq\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\big\|^{2}_{\nu}-\eta\sum_{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\big\|^{2}_{\nu}-C_{\eta}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\big\|^{2}_{\nu},

and

\displaystyle\left\langle w^{2|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}(\mathbf{K}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}),\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq\left(\eta\sum_{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\big\|_{\nu}+C_{\eta}\big\|\partial^{\alpha}\mathbf{f}_{n}\big\|_{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 273.14662pt\times\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\big\|_{\nu}.

Since
|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|=1
, the fourth term in (
4.4
) is bounded by

\displaystyle C\left\langle w^{2|\tilde{\beta}|}(\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}v_{j})\partial^{j}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1},\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91684pt\leq\iint_{(\mathbb{R}^{3})^{2}}w^{2|\tilde{\beta}|}\left|(\partial^{j}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}:\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1})\right|dvdx

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91684pt\leq\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}+|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\times\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}+(|\tilde{\beta}|-1)}\partial^{j}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91684pt\leq\eta\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\big\|_{\nu}^{2}+C_{\eta}\big\|w^{(|\tilde{\beta}|-1)}\partial^{j}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}_{\tilde{\beta}-\tilde{\beta}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\big\|^{2}_{\nu},

where
\|\cdot\|_{\nu}
is equivalent to
\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}
because of
w=(1+|v|)^{\gamma}
.

According to expression (
3.1
) for
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma_{\text{loss}}[f^{n},f^{n+1}]
and
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma_{\text{gain}}[f^{n},f^{n+1}]
, we apply Lemma
3.1
with
l=|\beta|
, then it holds that

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\left\langle w^{2|\tilde{\beta}|}\Big(\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{gain}}[f_{n}^{\alpha},f_{n}^{\beta}]-\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{loss}}[f_{n+1}^{\alpha},f_{n}^{\beta}]\Big),\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{n+1}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\left[\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\right\|_{\nu}\Big)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\right\|_{\nu}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+\left.\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\|_{\nu}\Big)\right]\times\left\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\right\|_{\nu}

where we have chosen
l-|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|\leq|\tilde{\beta}_{2}|
in Lemma
3.1
, with
\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\leq\tilde{\alpha}
,
\tilde{\beta}_{1}\leq\tilde{\beta}
, and
|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N
.

By (
1.9
), we notice that

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}[\mathbf{f}(s)]

then integrating (
4.4
) over [0, t], we derive

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(t)\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\int_{0}^{t}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}ds-\frac{1}{2}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(0)\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt\leq\eta\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{\big|\tilde{\beta}_{1}\big|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}\left(\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}+\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}\right)ds

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07182pt+C_{\eta}\sum_{\left|\tilde{\alpha}_{j}\right|\leq|\tilde{\alpha}|}\sum_{\left|\tilde{\beta}_{j}\right|<|\tilde{\beta}|}\int_{0}^{t}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{j}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{j}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{j}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}ds+C_{\eta}\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07182pt+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\right)\sum_{\left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\leq|\tilde{\alpha}|}\sum_{\left|\tilde{\beta}_{1}\right|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|_{\nu}+\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\big\|_{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 270.30118pt\times\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|_{\nu}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07182pt+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\right)\sum_{\left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\leq|\tilde{\alpha}|}\sum_{\left|\tilde{\beta}_{1}\right|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}\int_{0}^{t}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\big\|_{\nu}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|_{\nu}ds,

(4.5)

where
|\tilde{\alpha}_{j}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{j}|\leq N
,
\left|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\right|\leq|\tilde{\alpha}|
. Since
|\tilde{\beta}_{j}|<|\tilde{\beta}|
,
\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}
can be estimated only in term of the
x
derivatives
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}
via an induction starting from
|\tilde{\beta}|=1,2,\dots
in (
4.5
). Through this induction process, one gets

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(t)\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\int_{0}^{t}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(0)\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 22.76228pt+C\eta\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{\left|\tilde{\beta}_{1}\right|\leq|\tilde{\beta}|}\left(\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}+\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}\right)ds

\displaystyle\hskip 22.76228pt+C_{\eta}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{j}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{t}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{j}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}ds+C_{\eta}\int_{0}^{t}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 22.76228pt+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s))\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{n}(s))+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n}(s))\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s))

where
|\tilde{\alpha}_{j}|\leq N
,
\tilde{\beta}_{1}\leq\tilde{\beta}
,
\tilde{\alpha}_{1}\leq\tilde{\alpha}
, and the inequality

\int_{0}^{t}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|^{2}ds\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}(s))

is used to estimate the last two terms in (
4.5
). Summing over
|\tilde{\alpha}|+|\tilde{\beta}|\leq N
,
(\tilde{\beta}\neq 0)
, then it yields that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|+|\tilde{\beta}|\leq N}\left(\frac{1}{2}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(t)\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\int_{0}^{t}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}ds\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt\leq\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|+|\tilde{\beta}|\leq N}\left(C\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(0)\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\eta\int_{0}^{t}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}ds\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt\quad+C_{\eta}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}+\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}\right)ds

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt\quad+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s))\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{n}(s))+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n}(s))\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)).

(4.6)

Thus we complete the estimate for the mixed
x,v
- derivatives.

The second step is to deal with
x
-derivatives of
\mathbf{f}_{n+1}
. Taking
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}
of (
4.2
) gives

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}f_{n+1}^{\alpha}+\nu^{\alpha}(\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}f_{n+1}^{\alpha})+K^{\alpha}(\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\sum_{\beta=A,B}\Big(\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{gain}}[f^{\alpha}_{n},f^{\beta}_{n}]-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{loss}}[f^{\alpha}_{n+1},f^{\beta}_{n}]\Big).

(4.7)

By Lemma
3.1
with
l=0
, the nonlinear collision terms are bounded by

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\left\langle\nu^{\alpha}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}f_{n+1}^{\alpha},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}f_{n+1}^{\alpha}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\left\langle(\mathbf{K}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}),\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\left\langle\big(\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{gain}}[f_{n}^{\alpha},f_{n}^{\beta}]-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}_{\text{loss}}[f_{n+1}^{\alpha},f_{n}^{\beta}]\big),\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}f^{\alpha}_{n+1}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07182pt\leq C\left[\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq|\tilde{\alpha}|}\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\right\|_{\nu}\Big)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 48.36958pt+\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq|\tilde{\alpha}|}\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\right\|_{\nu}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 48.36958pt+\left.\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq|\tilde{\alpha}|}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\big\|_{\nu}\Big)\right]\times\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}\big\|_{\nu}.

Integrating the above equation over
[0,t]
leads to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}-\frac{1}{2}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(0)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\|^{2}_{\nu}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{n}(s))\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq|\tilde{\alpha}|}\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\right\|_{\nu}+\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\right\|_{\nu}\Big)\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\|_{\nu}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\quad+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s))\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq|\tilde{\alpha}|}\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\right\|_{\nu}\Big)\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\|_{\nu}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\quad-\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle(\mathbf{K}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n})(s),\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}ds,

(4.8)

We notice from (
2.1
) that

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle(\mathbf{K}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n})(s),\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}ds\Big|

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\right\|^{2}_{\nu}ds

\displaystyle\leq Ct\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left(\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(s)\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right).

Together with (
4.6
), this implies that for sufficiently small
\eta

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n+1}(t))

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{0})+Ct\left(\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n+1})(s)+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n})(s)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n+1})(s)\times\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{n})(s)

\displaystyle\quad+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n})(s)\times\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{n+1})(s)

So, the last term is bounded by

\frac{1}{4}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n+1})(s)+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{n})(s)

Let
\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n}(s))\leq 2C_{0}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{0})
. Solving
\mathcal{E}(f^{n+1}(t))
from above yields

\left(\frac{3}{4}-CT^{*}-C\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{0})\right)\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{n+1})(t)\leq\left(C_{0}+C\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{0})\right)\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{f}_{0})

where
T^{*}
and
\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}_{0})
are chosen sufficiently small. Therefore, this lemma follows.

∎

Theorem 4.2
.

For any small
M>0
, there exists
T^{*}(M)>0
and
M_{1}>0
, such that if the initial data satisfies

\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}})=\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|+|\tilde{\beta}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}_{\tilde{\beta}}\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}\big\|_{2}^{2}\leq M_{1},

then there is a unique solution
\mathbf{f}(t,x,v)
to equations (
1.23
), such that

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(t)\big]\leq M,

where
\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(t)\big]
is continuous over
[0,T^{*}(M))
. If
\mathbf{F}^{\rm{in}}(x,v)=\bm{\mu}+(\bm{\sqrt{\mu}}:\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}})\geq 0
, then

\mathbf{F}(t,x,v)=\bm{\mu}+(\bm{\sqrt{\mu}}:\mathbf{f})\geq 0.

Furthermore, for
\beta\neq 0
, the following energy estimate holds:

\displaystyle\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|+|\tilde{\beta}|\leq N}\left(\frac{1}{2}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t)\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\int_{0}^{t}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}ds\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|+|\tilde{\beta}|\leq N}\left(\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\int_{0}^{t}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}ds\right)+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big].

(4.9)

Proof.

Let
\mathbf{F}_{0}(t,x,v)\equiv\mathbf{F}^{\rm{in}}(x,v)
. By taking
n\to\infty
in Lemma
4.1
, we can obtain a classical solution
\mathbf{f}
, such that
\mathbf{F}(t,x,v)=\bm{\mu}+(\bm{\sqrt{\mu}}:\mathbf{f})\geq 0
.

To discuss the uniqueness of the solution, we suppose there is ‌another solution
\mathbf{g}
, satisfying
\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}\mathcal{E}(\mathbf{g})\leq M
. Under the notation (
1.25
), (
1.27
), taking the difference of
\mathbf{f}
and
\mathbf{g}
leads to

\left(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\right)[\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g}]+\bm{L}[\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g}]=\bm{\Gamma}[\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g},\mathbf{f}]+\bm{\Gamma}[\mathbf{g},\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g}],

(4.10)

with

\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}(x,v)=\mathbf{g}^{\rm{in}}(x,v)

Notice that there are no derivatives on the right-hand side in (
4.10
), we apply (
3.2
) and (
3.3
) in Lemma
3.1
with
\theta=0
to obtain

\displaystyle\big\langle\bm{\Gamma}[\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g},\mathbf{f}]+\bm{\Gamma}[\mathbf{g},\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g}],\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07182pt\leq C\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|+|\tilde{\beta}|\leq 4}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{g}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\big\|\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g}\big\|_{\nu}^{2}.

By (
2.1
) with
l=0
, it holds that

\left\langle\mathbf{L}[\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g}],\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\geq-C\|\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g}\|_{\nu}^{2},

Taking the
L^{2}
inner product of both sides of equations (
4.10
) with the vector
(\mathbf{f}-\mathbf{g})
leads to

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{f}(t)-\mathbf{g}(t)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big\|\mathbf{f}(s)-\mathbf{g}(s)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq(C\sqrt{M}+C)\int_{0}^{t}\big\|\mathbf{f}(s)-\mathbf{g}(s)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\int_{0}^{t}\big\|\mathbf{f}(s)-\mathbf{g}(s)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}ds.

By definition (
1.9
),
\mathbf{f}\equiv\mathbf{g}
follows directly by Gronwall’s inequality.

To prove the continuity of

\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(t)\big]
with respect to
t
, take the limit
n\to\infty
in both (
4.5
) and (
4.8
) to get

\Big|\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(t)\big]-\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\Big|\leq C\left(1+\sup_{s\leq\tau\leq t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(\tau)\big]}\right)\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|+|\tilde{\beta}|\leq 4}\int_{s}^{t}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(\tau)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}d\tau\to 0,

as
t\to s
, since
\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(\tau)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}
is integrable in
\tau
.

Finally, taking the limit as
n\to\infty
in (
4.6
) yields (
4.9
).
∎

The energy estimate derived in (
4.9
) is very important because it shows the necessity of controlling the term
\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}ds
, while also revealing the need for more refined estimates to spatial derivatives.

Lemma 4.3
.

Assume
\mathbf{f}(t,x,v)
satisfies equations (
1.23
) for
0\leq t\leq T
with

\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|+|\tilde{\beta}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq M,

(4.11)

for
0<M\leq 1
small. Then there exists some
C>0
such that for
0\leq s\leq t\leq T
,

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{s}^{t}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(\tau)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}d\tau\leq e^{C(t-s)}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

(4.12)

Moreover,

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\leq e^{C(t-s)}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

(4.13)

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{s}^{t}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(\tau)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}d\tau\geq C\left[1-e^{-C(t-s)}\right]\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(4.14)

Proof.

First, we prove (
4.12
). Notice that

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t)\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\left\langle\mathbf{L}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}=\left\langle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\bm{\Gamma}[\mathbf{f},\mathbf{f}],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

(4.15)

Since
L
is a non-negative operator satisfying (
4.11
), we apply (
3.2
) and (
3.3
) with
l=0
and
\mathbf{f}_{1}=\mathbf{f}_{2}=\mathbf{f}_{3}=\mathbf{f}
to get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{1}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{1}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Bigg)

(4.16)

\displaystyle\hskip 102.43008pt\times\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{2}|\leq\tilde{\alpha}}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{\nu}\Bigg)\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{M}\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{2}|\leq\tilde{\alpha}}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{\nu}\Bigg)\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{\nu}.

(4.17)

Summing over
|{\tilde{\alpha}}|\leq N
yields

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\frac{d}{dt}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\leq C\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Then, by the Gronwall’s inequality,

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\leq e^{C(t-s)}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Substituting this estimate into the right-hand side of (
4.12
) completes the proof.

To establish (
4.13
), we observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big\langle w\mathbf{L}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt=\big\langle w\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma\big[\mathbf{f},\mathbf{f}\big],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\leq\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Bigg)\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{2}|\leq\tilde{\alpha}}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{\nu}\Bigg)\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\leq C\sqrt{M}\Bigg(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{2}|\leq\tilde{\alpha}}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{\nu}\Bigg)\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{\nu},

(4.18)

by (
3.2
) and (
3.3
) with
l=\frac{1}{2}
. Notice from (
2.1
), we have

\left|\left\langle w\mathbf{L}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right|\leq C\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|^{2}_{\nu}

Then we obtain

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\frac{d}{dt}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\leq C\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{\nu}^{2}\leq C\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

Consequently, (
4.13
) is a direct result of Gronwall’s lemma.

It remains to prove (
4.14
). We integrate (
4.18
) over
[s,t]
to obtain

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}&\geq\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}-\int_{s}^{t}\left\langle w\mathbf{L}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}d\tau\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt-\int_{s}^{t}\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|+|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}_{i}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}_{i}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{i}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big)\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\Big(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{2}|\leq\tilde{\alpha}}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{\nu}\Big)\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\big\|_{\nu}d\tau\\
&\geq\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}-[C+\sqrt{M}]\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{s}^{t}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(\tau)\big\|^{2}_{\nu}d\tau\\
&\geq\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}-C\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{s}^{t}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(\tau)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}d\tau\end{aligned}

Finally, letting

\mathbf{G}(t)\equiv\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{s}^{t}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(\tau)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}d\tau

it follows that

\frac{d}{dt}\mathbf{G}(t)=\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}\geq C\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\geq\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}-C\mathbf{G}(t)

because
\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t)\|_{\nu}^{2}
is equivalent to
\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}
. The proof of (
4.14
) is completed by integrating the above first-order differential inequality across
[s,t]
.
∎

5.
Positivity of
\int_{0}^{1}\big\langle\mathbf{L}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\,ds

In this section, we are going to establish the positivity of
\int_{0}^{1}\big\langle\mathbf{L}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\,ds
for the solution
\mathbf{f}(t,x,v)
to the Boltzmann equations (
1.23
). Additionally, it is important to emphasize that conservation laws prove crucial in the proof process.

Lemma 5.1
.

Assume
\mathbf{f}(t,x,v)
satisfies (
1.23
) for
0\leq t\leq T
,
(T\geq 1)
. Suppose that the initial data
\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}(x,v)
satisfies the conservation laws (
1.29
), and the small amplitude assumption (
4.11
) is also valid for sufficiently small
0<M<1
. Then there exists a constant
0<\delta_{M}<1
, such that

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\big\langle\mathbf{L}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\,ds\geq\delta_{M}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\nu}^{2}\,ds.

(5.1)

We prove by contradiction. Assume that this lemma’s conclusion does not hold, then there exists a sequence of solutions
\mathbf{f}_{n}(t,x,v)
to (
1.23
), such that for
0\leq t\leq T
,

\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|+|\tilde{\beta}|\leq N}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq M,

and

0\leq\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\big\langle\mathbf{L}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\,ds\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\|_{\nu}^{2}\,ds.

Now, we define the normalized sequence of
\mathbf{Z}_{n}=(Z^{A}_{n},Z^{B}_{n})^{T}
, where

Z^{\alpha}_{n}(t,x,v)\equiv\frac{f^{\alpha}_{n}(t,x,v)}{\sqrt{\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\|_{\nu}^{2}\,ds}}.

(5.2)

So we have

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(s)\|_{\nu}^{2}\,ds=1,

(5.3)

which leads to

0\leq\int_{0}^{1}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\langle\mathbf{L}[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(s)],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(s)\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\,ds\leq\frac{1}{n}.

(5.4)

By Lemma
4.3
, it yields that

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(t)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\leq C\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(0)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},

and

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\big\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\big\|_{\nu}^{2}ds\geq C\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(0)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

We know from (
5.2
) and (
5.3
) that

\sup_{0\leq t\leq 1}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\big\|w^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\leq C,

(5.5)

which is uniform in
n
. Since
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}
satisfies

(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}+\mathbf{L}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\big]\equiv\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\bm{\Gamma}\big[\mathbf{f}_{n},\mathbf{f}_{n}\big].

We divide the above equations by
\sqrt{\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(s)\|_{\nu}^{2}\,ds}
to obtain

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})\partial^{\alpha}\mathbf{Z}_{n}+L[\partial^{\alpha}\mathbf{Z}_{n}]

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt=\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|\leq N/2}C_{\tilde{\alpha}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\Gamma[\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n},\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}]+\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|>N/2}C_{\tilde{\alpha}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\Gamma[\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\mathbf{Z}_{n},\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}].

(5.6)

Moreover,
\mathbf{Z}_{n}
also satisfies the conservation laws:

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big\langle\mathbf{Z}_{n},(\mathbf{e}_{i}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}dx

\displaystyle=0,\quad i=1,2,

(5.7)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big\langle\mathbf{Z}_{n},(v_{j}\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}dx

\displaystyle=0,\quad j=1,2,3,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big\langle\mathbf{Z}_{n},(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}dx

\displaystyle=0.

where
\bm{\mu}=(\mu^{A},\mu^{B})^{T}
,
\mathbf{m}=(m^{A},m^{B})^{T}
, and
\mathbf{e}_{j}
is the
j^{th}
unit vector in
\mathbb{R}^{2}
.

From (
4.11
) and (
5.5
), we know that both
\{\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v)\}
and
\{\mathbf{f}_{n}(t,x,v)\}
have weakly convergent subsequences, denoted as

\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v)
and
\mathbf{f}(t,x,v)
respectively. It is easy to verify that their weak limits satisfy

\partial^{\alpha}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v)\rightharpoonup\partial^{\alpha}\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v)\hskip 14.22636pt\text{as}\hskip 14.22636ptn\to\infty,

weakly with respect to the inner product
\int_{0}^{1}\langle\mathbf{f}(s),\mathbf{f}(s)\rangle_{\nu}ds
, and

w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{n}(t,x,v)\rightharpoonup w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(t,x,v)\hskip 14.22636pt\text{as}\hskip 14.22636ptn\to\infty,

weakly with respect to the function space
\big[L^{2}([0,1]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\big]^{2}
.

The subsequent proof is divided into two main steps.
The first step is to prove

\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v)=(\mathbf{a}(t,x):\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})+(\mathbf{b}(t,x)\cdot v)(\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})+c(t,x)|v|^{2}(\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})

where
\mathbf{a}=(a^{A},a^{B})^{T}
,
\mathbf{b}=(b_{1},b_{2},b_{3})^{T}
are two vectors. We also split
\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v)
by

\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v)=P_{0}\mathbf{Z}_{n}+\{I-P_{0}\}\mathbf{Z}_{n}=\sum_{j=1}^{6}\big\langle\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,\cdot),\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}(v)+\{I-P_{0}\}\mathbf{Z}_{n},

where
\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}(v)
is a orthonormal basis of
\mathbb{R}^{3}(v)
defined in (
1.20
). So it also holds that

\displaystyle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v)

\displaystyle=P_{0}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}+\{I-P_{0}\}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{6}\big\langle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v),\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}(v)+\{I-P_{0}\}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}.

(5.8)

By (
5.4
) and Lemma
2.2
, as
n\rightarrow\infty
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\sum_{|{\tilde{\alpha}}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\left\|\{I-P_{0}\}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}ds\leq\frac{1}{\delta}\sum_{|{\tilde{\alpha}}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\big\langle\mathbf{L}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}\big],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}ds\to 0.

(5.9)

To show that
\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v)
is not identically zero, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq j\leq 6}\int_{0}^{1}\left\|\left\langle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n},\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}-\left\langle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z},\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}ds\to 0.

(5.10)

This reduces to proving that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\left\langle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n},\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}-\left\langle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z},\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right|^{2}dxds\to 0.

(5.11)

Proof of (
5.11
).

For any
1>\eta>0
, we define a smooth cutoff function
in
[0,1]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}

\chi_{1}(t,x,v)\equiv 1,\hskip 14.22636pt\text{if}\hskip 5.69046pt(t,x,v)\in[\eta,1-\eta]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\times(|v|\leq\frac{1}{\eta}).

Then we split
\big\langle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v),\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}
into

\left\langle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v),\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=\big\langle(1-\chi_{1})\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v),\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}+\big\langle\chi_{1}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v),\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}.

(5.12)

For the first term above, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\left|\left\langle(1-\chi_{1})\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n},\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right|^{2}+\left|\left\langle(1-\chi_{1})\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z},\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\right|^{2}dx\,ds

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\int_{0}^{1}\iint_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1-\chi_{1})|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}|^{2}|\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}|\,dv\,dx\,dt

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+C\int_{0}^{1}\iint_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1-\chi_{1})|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}|^{2}|\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}|\,dv\,dx\,dt

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\Bigg\{\iiint_{0\leq s\leq\eta}+\iiint_{1-\eta\leq s\leq 1}+\iiint_{|v|\geq 1/\eta}\Bigg\}\Big[\big(|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}|^{2}+|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}|^{2}\big)(1-\chi_{1})|\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}|\Big]dv\,dx\,dt.

(5.13)

Since
\sup_{s}\big\{\|w^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}_{n}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|w^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\big\}\leq C
, and (
5.5
), it holds that

|\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}(v)|\leq o(\eta)(1+|v|)^{\gamma},\quad\text{for }|v|\geq 1/\eta,

so (
5.13
) can be further bounded by

C\eta\times\sup_{s}\iint_{\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1+|v|)^{\gamma}\big(|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(s,x,v)|^{2}+|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}(s,x,v)|^{2}\big)dv\,dx\leq C\eta.

(5.14)

Next, we need to prove that the second term
\left\langle\chi_{1}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v),\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}
in (
5.12
) is uniformly bounded in
\big[H^{\frac{1}{4}}([0,1]\times\mathbb{T}^{3})\big]^{2}
. Notice by (
5.6
),
\chi_{1}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}
satisfies

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})[\chi_{1}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}]

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt=-\chi_{1}\mathbf{L}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}\big]+\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})\chi_{1}+\sum_{\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}C_{\tilde{\alpha}}^{\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}\bm{\Gamma}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}\mathbf{f}_{n},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}\big]\chi_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 54.06006pt+\sum_{\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}C_{\tilde{\alpha}}^{\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}\Gamma[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}\mathbf{Z}_{n},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}\mathbf{f}_{n}]\chi_{1}.

(5.15)

To show that each term on the right-hand side of (
5.15
) is uniformly bounded in
\big[L^{2}([0,1]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\big]^{2}
. Firstly, it can be observed that

\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})\chi_{1}\in\big[L^{2}([0,1]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\big]^{2}.

Together with (
4.11
) and (
5.3
), and using (
3.5
) - (
3.8
), we obtain that

\left(\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}|\leq N/2}\Gamma[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}\mathbf{f}_{n},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}]+\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}|>N/2}\Gamma[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}\mathbf{Z}_{n},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}^{\prime}}\mathbf{f}_{n}]\right)\chi_{1}

is uniformly bounded in
\big[L^{2}([0,1]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\big]^{2}
. Moreover, by (
1.26
),

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|\chi_{1}L^{\alpha}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}\big]\big|^{2}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\sum_{\beta=A,B}\Bigg(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big|\chi_{1}\nu^{\alpha}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}Z^{\alpha}_{n}\big|^{2}dv+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}(v)}\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}Z_{n}^{\beta}(v_{*})dv_{*}\Big|^{2}\chi_{1}^{2}\,dv

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59596pt+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})}

\displaystyle\hskip 150.79968pt\times\Big[\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}Z^{\beta}_{n}(v_{*}^{\prime})+\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}Z_{n}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})\Big]d\omega dv_{*}^{\prime}\Big|^{2}\chi_{1}^{2}(v)dv\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\sum_{\beta=A,B}\Bigg(\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}Z^{\alpha}_{n}\big|_{\nu}^{2}+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}-v|^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}(v)}\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})}\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}Z_{n}^{\beta}(v_{*})\big|^{2}\chi_{1}^{2}\,dv_{*}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 45.52458pt+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{*}^{\prime}-v^{\prime}|^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})}\Big[\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}Z^{\beta}_{n}(v_{*}^{\prime})\big|^{2}+\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}Z_{n}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})\big|^{2}\Big]\chi_{1}^{2}(v)dv_{*}^{\prime}dv^{\prime}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq C\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}\big|_{\nu}^{2},

where
\chi_{1}
has compact support.

So, the right-hand side in (
5.15
) is uniformly bounded in
\big[L^{2}([0,1]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\big]^{2}
. Applying the averaging lemma in
[
8
]
, it yields that

\big\langle\chi_{1}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v),\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\chi_{1}(t,x,v)\big(\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v):\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}(v)\big)\,dv\,\in\big[H^{\frac{1}{4}}([0,1]\times\mathbb{T}^{3})\big]^{2}

which is uniform in
n
. Notice by (
4.11
) and
\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}\|_{\nu}^{2}ds=1
, we deduce that

\big\langle\chi_{1}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}(t,x,v),\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\to\big\langle\chi_{1}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v),\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\hskip 14.22636pt\text{ in }\hskip 5.69046pt\big[L^{2}([0,1]\times\mathbb{T}^{3})\big]^{2}.

The proofs of (
5.10
) and (
5.11
) follow by combining this with (
4.11
) and (
5.14
).
∎

Consequently, it holds that

\int_{0}^{1}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}\|_{\nu}^{2}\,ds\to 0.

Then we have

\int_{0}^{1}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}\|_{\nu}^{2}\,ds=1.

(5.16)

Letting
n\to\infty
in (
5.8
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v)

\displaystyle=P_{0}\mathbf{Z}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{6}\big\langle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v),\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\tilde{\bm{\chi}}_{j}(v)

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{a}(t,x):\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})+(\mathbf{b}(t,x)\cdot v)(\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})+c(t,x)|v|^{2}(\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}}).

Notice that

(\mathbf{e}_{1}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}}),\hskip 8.5359pt(\mathbf{e}_{2}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}}),\hskip 8.5359ptv_{i}(\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})\,(i=1,2,3),\hskip 8.5359pt|v|^{2}(\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}}),

these six vectors above are linearly independent. Therefore,
\mathbf{a}(t,x),\,\mathbf{b}(t,x),\,c(t,x)
can be expressed as a linear combinations of

\big\langle\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v),(\mathbf{e}_{1}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\,\hskip 14.22636pt\big\langle\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v),v_{i}(\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\,i=1,2,3,

and

\big\langle\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v),(\mathbf{e}_{2}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\,\hskip 14.22636pt\big\langle\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v),|v|^{2}(\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}.

By (
5.5
), we deduce that

\sup_{0\leq t\leq 1}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\left[\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{a}(t)\big|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{b}(t)\big|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}c(t)\big|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\right]\leq C.

(5.17)

To derive the equations for the limiting function
\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v)
, it is essential to prove

\displaystyle\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq N/2}\bm{\Gamma}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}\big]+\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|>N/2}\bm{\Gamma}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z}_{n},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\to\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq N/2}\bm{\Gamma}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z}\big]+\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|>N/2}\bm{\Gamma}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}\big]\quad\text{in }\mathcal{D}^{\prime}.

(5.18)

Proof of (
5.18
).

We split (
5.18
) into two parts:

\displaystyle\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq N/2}\bm{\Gamma}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z}\big]+\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|>N/2}\bm{\Gamma}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z}_{n}-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}\big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq N/2}\bm{\Gamma}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z}\big]+\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|>N/2}\bm{\Gamma}\big[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z}_{n},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}\big].

By (
5.16
) and (
3.5
), the first two terms converge to zero in
\mathcal{D}^{\prime}
.

Since
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}
and
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}_{n}-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}
converge weakly in
\big[L^{2}([0,1]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})\big]^{2}
, the last two terms also converge to zero in
\mathcal{D}^{\prime}
. We first choose an
\eta
‌sufficiently small, and then let
n\to\infty
in Lemma
3.3
. Consequently, (
5.18
) follows.
∎

Letting
n\to\infty
in (
5.6
) and separating the linear parts from the nonlinear parts yields

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}

\displaystyle\hskip 36.98866pt=\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|\leq N/2}C_{\tilde{\alpha}}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\Gamma[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z}]+\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}_{1}|>N/2}C_{\alpha}^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\Gamma[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{Z},\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}-\tilde{\alpha}_{1}}\mathbf{f}]

\displaystyle\hskip 36.98866pt=:\mathbf{h}_{\tilde{\alpha}},

(5.19)

Moreover, the conservation laws also hold as:

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big\langle\mathbf{Z},(\mathbf{e}_{i}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}dx

\displaystyle=0,\quad i=1,2,

(5.20)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big\langle\mathbf{Z},(v_{j}\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}dx

\displaystyle=0,\quad j=1,2,3,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\big\langle\mathbf{Z},(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})\big\rangle_{L_{v}^{2}}dx

\displaystyle=0.

The second step is to prove

\int_{0}^{1}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{Z}(s)\|^{2}_{\nu}\,ds\leq CM.

If we choose
M
sufficiently small, this will lead to a contradiction with (
5.16
). After substituting

\mathbf{Z}(t,x,v)=(\mathbf{a}(t,x):\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})+(\mathbf{b}(t,x)\cdot v)(\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})+c(t,x)|v|^{2}(\mathbf{m}:\sqrt{\bm{\mu}})

into equations (
5.19
), we obtain

\displaystyle m^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}c\cdot v|v|^{2}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}+\{m^{\alpha}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{c}|v|^{2}+m^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{b}\cdot v)\}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}

(5.21)

\displaystyle+\{m^{\alpha}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{b}+\nabla_{x}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}a^{\alpha}\}\cdot v\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}+\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{a^{\alpha}}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}=h^{\alpha}_{\tilde{\alpha}}(\mathbf{f},\mathbf{Z}),

where
\alpha\in\{A,B\},\,
\mathbf{a}(t,x)=(a^{A},a^{B})^{T}(t,x)
and
|\alpha|\leq N
. The dot
`\cdot^{\prime}

represents Newton’s notation for the derivative with respect to time.
Since
\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}},\ v_{i}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}},\ v_{i}^{2}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}},\ v_{i}v_{j}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}},\ v_{i}|v|^{2}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}
are linearly independent,
this set of vectors can be transformed into an orthonormal basis
\hat{\bm{\chi}}_{k}^{\alpha}
via Gram-Schmidt orthogonalization, which satisfies

[\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}},v_{i}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}},v_{i}^{2}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}},v_{i}v_{j}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}},|v|^{2}v_{i}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}]A^{\alpha}_{13\times 13}=[\hat{\bm{\chi}}_{k}^{\alpha}],

‌where
1\leq i\neq j\leq 3,\,1\leq k\leq 13
and
A^{\alpha}_{13\times 13}
is the matrix representing the linear transformation with
\det A^{\alpha}_{13\times 13}\neq 0
.
Then we compare the coefficients of
\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}
,
v_{i}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}
,
v_{i}^{2}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}
,
v_{i}v_{j}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}
and
|v|^{2}v_{i}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}
, on both sides of (
5.21
) to obtain

\displaystyle m^{\alpha}\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}c=

\displaystyle h_{ci,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},

(5.22)

\displaystyle m^{\alpha}\{\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{c}+\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}\}=

\displaystyle h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},

(5.23)

\displaystyle m^{\alpha}\{\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{j}+\partial^{j}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}\}=

\displaystyle h_{ij,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},\quad i\neq j,

(5.24)

\displaystyle m^{\alpha}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{b}_{i}+\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}a^{\alpha}=

\displaystyle h_{bi,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},

(5.25)

\displaystyle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{a^{\alpha}}=

\displaystyle h_{a,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},

(5.26)

where
h_{a,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},\ h_{bi,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},\ h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},\ h_{ij,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},\ h_{ci,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}
can be expressed as follows:

\left[h_{a,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},\ h_{bi,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},\ h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},\ h_{ij,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha},\ h_{ci,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}\right]=\left[\int_{\mathbf{R}^{3}}h^{\alpha}_{\tilde{\alpha}}(t,x,v)\hat{\bm{\chi}}_{k}^{\alpha}(v)dv\right]A.

Applying (
3.7
),(
3.8
) and (
5.17
) to
\mathbf{h}_{\tilde{\alpha}}
in (
5.19
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\sup_{0\leq s\leq 1}\Big\{|h_{a,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}(s)|_{L_{x}^{2}}+|h_{bi,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}(s)|_{L_{x}^{2}}+|h_{ci,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}(s)|_{L_{x}^{2}}+|h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}(s)|_{L_{x}^{2}}+|h_{ij,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}(s)|_{L_{x}^{2}}\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\sqrt{M}.

(5.27)

Next, we use equations (
5.22
)–(
5.26
) to show that
\mathbf{a}(t,x)
,
\mathbf{b}(t,x)
and
c(t,x)
are of the order
O(\sqrt{M})
. By (
5.22
),
\nabla\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}c(t,x)
is bounded by

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}|\nabla\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}c|_{L_{x}^{2}}\leq C\sum_{i}|h_{ci,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}|_{L_{x}^{2}}\leq C\sqrt{M}.

(5.28)

Now, we turn to
b_{i}(t,x)
, for
1\leq i\leq 3
. Taking
\partial^{i}
of (
5.23
) and
\partial^{j}
of (
5.24
) yields

\displaystyle m^{\alpha}\Delta\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}

\displaystyle=m^{\alpha}\sum_{j}\partial^{jj}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}

\displaystyle=m^{\alpha}\sum_{j\neq i}\partial^{jj}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}+m^{\alpha}\partial^{ii}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}

\displaystyle=m^{\alpha}\sum_{j\neq i}\left[-\partial^{ji}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{j}+\partial^{j}h_{ij,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}\right]+\left[\partial^{i}h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}-m^{\alpha}\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{c}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\neq i}\left[m^{\alpha}\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{c}-\partial^{i}h_{j,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}\right]-m^{\alpha}\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{c}+\sum_{j\neq i}\partial^{j}h_{ij,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}+\partial^{i}h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=2m^{\alpha}\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{c}-m^{\alpha}\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{c}-\sum_{j\neq i}\left[\partial^{i}h_{j,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}-\partial^{j}h_{ij,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}\right]+\partial^{i}h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=m^{\alpha}\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{c}-\sum_{j\neq i}\left[\partial^{i}h_{j,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}-\partial^{j}h_{ij,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}\right]+\partial^{i}h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=-m^{\alpha}\partial^{ii}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}+\partial^{i}h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}-\sum_{j\neq i}\left[\partial^{i}h_{j,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}-\partial^{j}h_{ij,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}\right]+\partial^{i}h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=-m^{\alpha}\partial^{ii}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}+2\partial^{i}h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}-\sum_{j\neq i}\left[\partial^{i}h_{j,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}-\partial^{j}h_{ij,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}\right].

Multiplying
\Delta\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}
with
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}
, then integrating over
\mathbb{T}^{3}
, we obtain

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\left|\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{b}\right|_{L_{x}^{2}}\leq C\sum_{i,j}\left[|h_{i,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}|_{L_{x}^{2}}+|h_{ij,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}|_{L_{x}^{2}}\right]\leq C\sqrt{M}.

(5.29)

An integration over a time interval is needed when estimating
\nabla\mathbf{a}(t,x)
. Without loss of generality, we assume
t\geq\frac{1}{2}
(otherwise, consider the time interval
[t,1]
). Integrating (
5.25
) over
[0,t]
yields,

\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}(t)-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}a^{\alpha}(s)ds=\int_{0}^{t}h_{bi,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}(s)ds.

(5.30)

where
0\leq|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N-1
. Since
\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{a^{\alpha}}=\partial^{i}h_{a,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}\in L^{2}([0,1]\times\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
,

\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}a^{\alpha}(s)=\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}a^{\alpha}(t)+\int_{t}^{s}\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{a^{\alpha}}(\tau)d\tau.

Plugging the above equation into (
5.30
),and integrating over
[0,t]
, we get

\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}a^{\alpha}(t)=-\frac{1}{t}\left[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}(t)-\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}(0)\right]-\frac{1}{t}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{t}^{s}\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\dot{a^{\alpha}}(\tau)d\tau ds+\frac{1}{t}\int_{0}^{t}h_{bi,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}ds.

(5.31)

Applying
\partial^{i}
again to both sides of equation (
5.31
), we obtain

\displaystyle\Delta\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}a^{\alpha}(t)=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{t}\sum_{i}\left[\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}(t)-\partial^{i}\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}b_{i}(0)\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{t}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{t}^{s}\Delta h_{a,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}(\tau)d\tau ds+\frac{1}{t}\sum_{i}\int_{0}^{t}\partial^{i}h_{bi,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}ds.

(5.32)

Multiplying
\Delta\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}a^{\alpha}(t)
with
\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}a^{\alpha}
, equation (
5.32
) implies

|\nabla\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}a^{\alpha}|_{L_{x}^{2}}\leq C\left[|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{b}|_{L_{x}^{2}}+|\nabla_{x}h_{a,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}|_{L_{x}^{2}}+|h_{bi,\tilde{\alpha}}^{\alpha}|_{L_{x}^{2}}\right].

Therefore, combining the above equality with (
5.28
), (
5.29
) and (
5.27
), yields

\sum_{\tilde{\alpha}\neq 0,|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\left[\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{a}\big|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{b}\big|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+\big|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}c\big|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}\right](s)ds\leq CM.

Finally, we estimate the
L^{2}_{x}
norm of
\mathbf{a}(t,x),\mathbf{b}(t,x)
and
c(t,x)
. Taking
\tilde{\alpha}=0
in (
5.22
)–(
5.26
), yields

\int_{0}^{1}\left[|\dot{\mathbf{a}}|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+|\dot{\mathbf{b}}|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+|\dot{c}|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}\right](s)ds\leq CM.

By the Poincaré inequality in
[0,1]\times\mathbf{T}^{3}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\Big[|\mathbf{a}|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+|\mathbf{b}|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+|c|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}\Big](s)ds

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq C\int_{0}^{1}\Big[|\nabla_{t,x}\mathbf{a}|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+|\nabla_{t,x}\mathbf{b}|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+|\nabla_{t,x}c|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}\Big](s)ds

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt+C|\mathbb{T}^{3}|\left(\Big|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{a}\,dtdx\Big|^{2}+\Big|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{b}\,dtdx\Big|^{2}+\Big|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}c\,dtdx\Big|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq CM+C|\mathbb{T}^{3}|\left(\Big|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{a}\,dtdx\Big|^{2}+\Big|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{b}\,dtdx\Big|^{2}+\Big|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}c\,dtdx\Big|^{2}\right).

But from the conservation laws in (
5.20
), we have
\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{b}\,dx\,dt=0
, and

\left|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathbf{a}(t,x)\,dx\,dt\right|+\left|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}c(t,x)\,dx\,dt\right|=0.

This implies that
\int_{0}^{1}\left[|\mathbf{a}(s)|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+|\mathbf{b}(s)|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+|c(s)|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}\right](s)ds\leq CM
, and therefore

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\left[|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{a}|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{b}|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}c|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}\right](s)ds\leq CM.

This is a contradiction to
\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{1}\|\mathbf{Z}\|_{\nu}^{2}\,ds=1
, if
M
is chosen to be small. This lemma thus follows.
\square

Proof of Theorem
1.2
. Assumption (
4.11
) is valid for
0\leq t\leq T_{*}
because

\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N}\left\||w|^{|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}f(s)\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\mathcal{E}(f(s))\leq M.

If
T_{*}\leq 1
, the conclusion follows naturally.
If
T_{*}>1
, there exists an integer
n\geq 1
such that
n<T_{*}\leq n+1
. We apply Lemma
5.1
to each interval
[0,1],[1,2],\ldots[n-1,n],[n,T_{*}]
.

6. Global existence

Proof of Theorem
1.1
. We choose
0\leq M\leq 1
and
8\leq N
, such that both Lemma
5.1
and Theorem
4.2
hold. Let the initial data satisfy

\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}]=\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|+|\tilde{\beta}|\leq N}\frac{1}{2}\big\|w^{|\tilde{\beta}|}\partial_{\tilde{\beta}}^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}_{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq M_{1}.

as in Theorem
4.2
. Let

T=\sup_{t}\left\{t:\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\leq M\right\}>0.

First, we estimate the
x
derivatives for
\mathbf{f}
. By (
4.8
) we get

\frac{1}{2}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}(t)+\int_{t_{1}}^{t}\langle[\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}ds\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\int_{0}^{t}\langle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\bm{\Gamma}[\mathbf{f},\mathbf{f}],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}ds.

For any
t=t_{1}+n
, where
0\leq t_{1}\leq 1
and
n
is an integer. By Theorem
1.2
, and the fact the linear operator
\mathbf{L}
is nonnegative in
[0,t_{1}]
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}(t)+\delta_{M}\int_{t_{1}}^{t}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\nu}^{2}ds+\delta_{M}\int_{0}^{t_{1}}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\nu}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}]+\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\langle\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\Gamma[\mathbf{f},\mathbf{f}],\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}ds+\delta_{M}\int_{0}^{t_{1}}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\nu}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}]+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}(s)]}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\nu}^{2}ds

\displaystyle+\delta_{M}\int_{0}^{t_{1}}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\nu}^{2}ds,

where
0<\delta_{M}<1
. From Lemma
4.3
with
t_{1}\leq 1
,

\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\int_{0}^{t_{1}}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\nu}^{2}ds\leq C\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

Therefore, there exists
C_{M}>0
such that

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\sum_{|\tilde{\alpha}|\leq N}\left[\frac{1}{2}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\int_{0}^{s}\|\partial^{\tilde{\alpha}}\mathbf{f}(s)\|^{2}_{\nu}ds\right]\leq C_{M}\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}]+C_{M}\left[\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\right]^{3/2}.

(5.33)

Substitute (
5.33
) into the right-hand side of (
4.9
) to get, for some other constant
C_{M}\geq 1
,

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\leq C_{M}\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}]+C_{M}\left[\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\right]^{3/2}.

(5.34)

To construct a global solution, for such chosen
M>0,C_{M}>0
, we further choose
M_{2}\leq M_{1}<M
such that

C_{M}M_{2}^{1/2}\leq\frac{1}{2}.

Finally let

\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}]\leq\varepsilon_{0}\equiv\frac{M_{2}}{4C_{M}}\leq\frac{M_{2}}{2}\leq\frac{M_{1}}{2}<\frac{M}{2},

and let
C_{0}=2C_{M}
. By Theorem
4.2
,

T_{2}=\sup_{t}\left\{t:\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\leq M_{2}\right\}>0.

Clearly we have
0\leq t\leq T_{2}\leq T
and
M_{2}^{1/2}C_{M}\leq 1/2
. It follows by (
5.34
) that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{2}}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\leq C_{M}\mathcal{E}(f_{0})+C_{M}\left[\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{2}}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\right]^{3/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C_{M}\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}]+\left[\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{2}}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\right]\left[C_{M}\left(\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{2}}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\right)^{1/2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C_{M}\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}]+\frac{1}{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{2}}[\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]].

Therefore,

\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{2}}\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]\leq 2C_{M}\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}]=C_{0}\mathcal{E}[\mathbf{f}^{\rm{in}}]\leq 2C_{M}\times\frac{M_{2}}{4C_{M}}=\frac{M_{2}}{2}<M_{2}.

(5.35)

Comparing (
5.35
) with the definition of
T_{2}
, implies
T_{2}=\infty
from the continuity of
\mathcal{E}\big[\mathbf{f}(s)\big]
. Consequently, we have completed the proof of Theorem
1.1
.
